# List 22

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>riveting</b></td><td>/ˈrɪvɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吸引人的，极迷人的</td><td>a riveting account<br>引人入胜的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary offers a riveting account of life inside the mountain villages.  这部纪录片对山村生活的叙述引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 absorbing</td><td>/əbˈzɔːrbɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，吸引人的</td><td>an absorbing novel<br>引人入胜的小说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I found her account of the voyage utterly absorbing.  我觉得她对航行的叙述极其引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arresting</td><td>/əˈrestɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吸引人的</td><td>an arresting image<br>引人注目的画面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poster used an arresting image of a burning city.  海报用了一幅燃烧城市的醒目画面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 engaging</td><td>/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有吸引力的，讨人喜欢的</td><td>an engaging personality<br>讨人喜欢的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His engaging manner made strangers feel comfortable within minutes.  他讨人喜欢的举止让陌生人几分钟内就放松下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 engrossing</td><td>/ɪnˈɡroʊsɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，令人专注的</td><td>an engrossing story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary was so engrossing that nobody left early.  这部纪录片如此引人入胜，没人提早离场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthralling</td><td>/ɪnˈθrɔlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人入迷的，极吸引人的</td><td>an enthralling performance<br>令人入迷的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her enthralling storytelling kept the children silent for an hour.  她迷人的讲述让孩子们安静了一个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fascinating</td><td>/ˈfæsəˌneɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷人的，极有趣的</td><td>a fascinating discovery<br>引人入胜的发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Insect behavior is a fascinating subject for young scientists.  昆虫行为对年轻科学家来说是极有趣的课题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immersing</td><td>/ɪˈmɜrsɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人沉浸的，投入的</td><td>an immersing experience<br>令人沉浸的体验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The game offers an immersing world full of hidden details.  这款游戏提供了充满隐藏细节的沉浸世界。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intriguing</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtriːɡɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人好奇的，有趣的</td><td>an intriguing question<br>引人好奇的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an intriguing point that nobody had considered.  她提出了一个无人考虑过的有趣观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involving</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlvɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，扣人心弦的</td><td>an involving story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary is more involving than any thriller this year.  这部纪录片比今年任何惊悚片都更扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insipid</td><td>/ɪnˈsɪpɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平淡的，无聊的</td><td>an insipid conversation<br>无聊的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film was so insipid that half the audience fell asleep.  这部电影平淡无聊，半数观众都睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vapid</td><td>/ˈvæpɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣的，乏味的</td><td>a vapid conversation<br>乏味的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party ended in vapid conversation about traffic and the weather.  聚会最后只剩下关于交通和天气的乏味闲谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>renaissance</b></td><td>/ˈrenəsɑːns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复兴</td><td>a cultural renaissance<br>文化复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The district is enjoying a cultural renaissance driven by young artists.  这个街区正经历由年轻艺术家推动的文化复兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regeneration</td><td>/rɪˌdʒenəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>重生，复兴；再生</td><td>urban regeneration<br>城市复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Urban regeneration brought shops back to the empty waterfront.  城市复兴让空荡的滨水区重新有了店铺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejuvenation</td><td>/rɪˌdʒuːvəˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恢复活力，返老还童</td><td>urban rejuvenation<br>城市复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The holiday brought a complete rejuvenation of his spirits.  假期让他的精神完全恢复了活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revival</td><td>/rɪˈvaɪvəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>复兴，复苏</td><td>a revival of interest<br>兴趣的重新兴起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neighborhood is enjoying a revival after decades of decline.  这个街区在数十年衰落后正迎来复兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resurgence</td><td>/rɪˈsɜːrdʒəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复兴</td><td>a resurgence of interest<br>兴趣的再度高涨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Recent films have caused a surprising resurgence of interest in classical music.  近期的电影令人们对古典音乐的兴趣意外复兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resurrection</td><td>/ˌrezəˈrekʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复活，复兴</td><td>the resurrection of hope<br>希望的重生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fans witnessed the resurrection of a club everyone had written off.  球迷们见证了一家人人都不看好的俱乐部的复兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resuscitation</td><td>/rɪˌsʌsɪˈteɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复苏，苏醒；急救</td><td>attempt resuscitation<br>尝试急救复苏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Paramedics performed resuscitation on the scene for ten minutes.  急救人员在现场进行了十分钟的复苏抢救。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revitalization</td><td>/ˌriːvaɪtələˈzeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复兴，重新振兴</td><td>urban revitalization<br>城市复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The festival began the revitalization of the sleepy river town.  这个节庆开启了沉寂河镇的复兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renewal</td><td>/rɪˈnuːəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复兴，更新；续期</td><td>spiritual renewal<br>精神上的更新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Spring always brings a feeling of renewal to the village.  春天总给这个村子带来一种更新的感觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>robust</b></td><td>/roʊˈbʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的；强壮的，健康的</td><td>robust good health<br>强健的好身体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains robust enough to work in the garden daily.  他八十岁了，身体依然强健，每天还能在园中劳作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bouncing</td><td>/ˈbaʊnsɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼健康的，精力充沛的</td><td>a bouncing baby boy<br>健康活泼的男婴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave birth to a bouncing baby boy last spring.  去年春天她生下一个健康活泼的男婴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dynamic</td><td>/daɪˈnæmɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有活力的，动态的</td><td>a dynamic leader<br>有活力的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city has a dynamic arts scene that keeps changing.  这座城市有着不断变化的活跃艺术圈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energetic</td><td>/ˌenərˈdʒetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的</td><td>an energetic performance<br>活力四射的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at seventy she remains remarkably energetic and full of plans.  即使七十岁了，她依然精力充沛，计划满满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gingerly</td><td>/ˈdʒɪndʒərli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>副词、形容词</td><td>小心谨慎地；谨慎的</td><td>step gingerly<br>小心翼翼地走</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gingerly lifted the cracked vase onto the padded table.  她小心翼翼地把破裂的花瓶搬到铺垫的桌上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hale</td><td>/heɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，健壮的</td><td>hale and hearty<br>身体强健，精神饱满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains hale enough to walk five miles a day.  八十岁的他依然强健，每天能走五英里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 potent</td><td>/ˈpoʊtənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强有力的，有效力的</td><td>a potent argument<br>有力的论据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fear of shame is a potent motive in small communities.  在小社群中，怕丢脸是一种强有力的动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strong</td><td>/strɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，坚固的</td><td>strong arms<br>强壮的臂膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge is strong enough to carry heavy trucks.  这座桥足够坚固，能承载重型卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sound</td><td>/saʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>牢固的；健康强壮的；（逻辑）严谨的</td><td>sound financial advice<br>稳妥的理财建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyer gave sound financial advice that saved the family thousands.  她的律师给出稳妥的理财建议，为家里省下数千元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vigorous</td><td>/ˈvɪɡərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力旺盛的，强有力的</td><td>vigorous exercise<br>剧烈运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors recommend thirty minutes of vigorous exercise daily.  医生建议每天进行三十分钟的高强度运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vital</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；极重要的</td><td>full of vital energy<br>充满生命活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise keeps older people vital and mentally alert.  经常锻炼使老年人保持活力和头脑清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wholesome</td><td>/ˈhoʊlsəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益身心健康的</td><td>wholesome home-cooked food<br>有益健康的家常饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children grew strong on wholesome food and plenty of fresh air.  孩子们靠有益健康的食物和充足的新鲜空气长得很结实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delicate</td><td>/ˈdeləkət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微妙的，精细的；脆弱的</td><td>a delicate distinction<br>微妙的区别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a delicate ear for the subtle differences between dialects.  她对方言之间的细微差别有很灵敏的耳朵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frail</td><td>/freɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；脆弱的</td><td>a frail old woman<br>体弱的老妇人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A frail old woman waited alone at the crowded bus stop.  一位虚弱的老妇人独自等在拥挤的公交站。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weak</td><td>/ˈwiːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脆弱的，虚弱的</td><td>a weak structure<br>脆弱的结构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge has a weak structure and cannot bear trucks.  这座桥结构脆弱，无法承载卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>roil</b></td><td>/rɔɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>roil the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The careless remarks of the mayor roiled the crowd outside the courthouse.  市长轻率的言论激怒了法院外的人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enrage</td><td>/enˈreɪdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使愤怒，激怒</td><td>enrage the audience<br>激怒观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flippant apology only enraged the families further.  他轻率的道歉只让那些家庭更加愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exasperate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæspəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒，激怒</td><td>exasperate his parents<br>使他的父母恼火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His constant lateness exasperated even the most patient teachers.  他总是迟到，连最有耐心的老师都被激怒了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incense</td><td>/ɪnˈsens/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>incense the public<br>激怒公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal is certain to incense feminists across the country.  这项提案肯定会激怒全国的女权主义者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inflame</td><td>/ɪnˈfleɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧</td><td>inflame public anger<br>使公众愤怒加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's careless comments only inflamed public anger over the housing shortage.  市长轻率的言论只是加剧了公众对住房短缺的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infuriate</td><td>/ɪnˈfjʊrieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>infuriate the critics<br>激怒批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision to cut pensions infuriated thousands of workers across the country.  削减养老金的决定激怒了全国成千上万的工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irritate</td><td>/ˈɪrɪteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒；刺激</td><td>irritate the customers<br>使顾客恼火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud advertisements before every video irritate most of our customers.  每个视频前的大声广告让我们大多数客户感到恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provoke</td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rile</td><td>/raɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，惹怒</td><td>rile up the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing riles him more than being interrupted in the middle of a sentence.  没有什么比话说到一半被打断更让他恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vex</td><td>/veks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使烦恼，使恼怒</td><td>vex the teacher<br>使老师恼怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant interruptions vexed the teacher throughout the long afternoon lesson.  不断的打扰让老师在漫长的下午课上十分恼怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gratify</td><td>/ˈɡrætɪfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使满足，使高兴</td><td>gratify a desire<br>满足某种欲望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It gratified her to see her students succeed.  看到学生成功让她很欣慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 please</td><td>/ˈpliːz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使高兴，使满意</td><td>please the crowd<br>取悦人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The simple ending pleased readers far more than critics expected.  这个简单的结局比评论家预料的更让读者满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rosy</b></td><td>/ˈroʊzi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观的</td><td>paint a rosy picture<br>描绘乐观前景</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their report paints a rosy picture that ignores the falling export figures.  他们的报告描绘出乐观前景，却忽视了下滑的出口数据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 auspicious</td><td>/ɔːˈspɪʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好兆头的</td><td>an auspicious beginning<br>吉利的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rain on the wedding day was considered an auspicious beginning.  婚礼当天下雨被认为是个好兆头的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encouraging</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人鼓舞的</td><td>encouraging early results<br>令人鼓舞的初步结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The first trial produced encouraging results for the new treatment.  首轮试验为这种新疗法带来了令人鼓舞的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heartening</td><td>/ˈhɑːrtənɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人振奋的</td><td>a heartening response<br>令人振奋的反响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was heartening to see strangers helping clear the flooded street.  看到陌生人一起清理被淹的街道令人振奋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 optimistic</td><td>/ˌɑːptəˈmɪstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观的</td><td>remain cautiously optimistic<br>保持谨慎乐观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite the losses, the founders remained optimistic about next year.  尽管亏损，创始人们对明年依然乐观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 promising</td><td>/ˈprɑːməsɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有前途的，有希望的</td><td>a promising young writer<br>有前途的青年作家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is the most promising violinist the school has ever trained.  她是这所学校培养过的最有前途的小提琴手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 propitious</td><td>/prəˈpɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吉祥的</td><td>a propitious moment<br>吉利的时机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They waited for a propitious moment to announce the merger.  他们等待一个吉利的时机来宣布合并。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bleak</td><td>/ˈbliːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（天气）阴冷的；荒凉的</td><td>a bleak winter day<br>阴冷的冬日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The moor looked bleak under grey November skies.  灰暗的十一月天空下，荒原显得阴冷荒凉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depressing</td><td>/dɪˈpresɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人沮丧的</td><td>a depressing forecast<br>令人沮丧的预测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rainy weather and empty streets felt deeply depressing.  阴雨天气和空荡的街道让人非常压抑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discouraging</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人沮丧的</td><td>discouraging news<br>令人沮丧的消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The discouraging test scores made him consider dropping the course.  令人沮丧的考试成绩让他考虑退掉这门课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gloomy</td><td>/ˈɡluːmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>a gloomy mood<br>忧郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew gloomy whenever the winter rain lasted more than a week.  每当冬雨连下一周以上，她就变得忧郁起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inauspicious</td><td>/ˌɪnɔːˈspɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不吉利的，不祥的</td><td>an inauspicious start<br>不吉利的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team made an inauspicious start by losing twice at home.  球队主场连输两场，开局不利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pessimistic</td><td>/ˌpesɪˈmɪstɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲观的</td><td>a pessimistic outlook<br>悲观的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained pessimistic about the company's chances of survival.  他对公司生存的机会仍然持悲观态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unpromising</td><td>/ʌnˈprɑːmɪsɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不被看好的，没希望的</td><td>an unpromising start<br>不被看好的开局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team made an unpromising start but finished the season strongly.  球队开局不被看好，赛季末却表现强势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unpropitious</del></td><td>/ˌʌnprəˈpɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不吉利的，不利的</td><td>an unpropitious start<br>不利的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project began under distinctly unpropitious circumstances.  该项目在明显不利的情况下启动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rookie</b></td><td>/ˈrʊki/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>新兵；新手；菜鸟</td><td>a rookie mistake<br>新手才犯的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rookie made a nervous throw that cost his team the game.  那名新手一次紧张的传球让球队输掉了比赛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apprentice</td><td>/əˈprentəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>学徒，新手</td><td>a carpenter's apprentice<br>木匠学徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He started as an apprentice and now owns the shop.  他从学徒做起，现在拥有这家店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fledgling</td><td>/ˈfledʒlɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>新手</td><td>a fledgling company<br>新兴的公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fledgling airline struggled through its very first winter.  这家新兴航空公司艰难度过了第一个冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 freshman</td><td>/ˈfreʃmən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大一新生；新手</td><td>a freshman senator<br>新任参议员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a freshman he knew almost nobody on campus.  作为大一新生，他在校园里几乎谁也不认识。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 greenhorn</td><td>/ˈɡriːnhɔrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>新手，生手</td><td>a complete greenhorn<br>十足的新手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a greenhorn he asked questions the veterans found obvious.  作为新手，他问的问题老手们觉得显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neophyte</td><td>/ˈniːəfaɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>初学者，新手</td><td>a neophyte in politics<br>政界的新手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a neophyte in politics, he underestimated how slowly legislation actually moves.  作为政界的新手，他低估了立法进程实际有多缓慢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 novice</td><td>/ˈnɑːvɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>新手，初学者</td><td>a complete novice<br>彻底的新手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A complete novice, he had never held a chisel before that afternoon.  他是个彻底的新手，那天下午之前从没握过凿子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recruit</td><td>/rəˈkruːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>新成员，新兵；招募</td><td>a raw recruit<br>毫无经验的新兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every new recruit spends six weeks in basic training.  每名新兵都要接受六周基础训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tyro</td><td>/ˈtaɪroʊ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>新手，业余爱好者</td><td>a tyro at chess<br>国际象棋新手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a tyro at chess, he lost every game that afternoon.  作为国际象棋新手，他那天下午每局都输了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 veteran</td><td>/ˈvetərən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>老兵；有丰富经验的人</td><td>a veteran journalist<br>资深记者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A veteran journalist, she has covered four wars and six elections.  作为资深记者，她报道过四场战争和六次选举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rote</b></td><td>/roʊt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>死记硬背</td><td>learn by rote<br>死记硬背地学</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Learning grammar by rote rarely helps students speak the language naturally.  死记硬背语法很少能帮学生自然地说这门语言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drill</td><td>/ˈdrɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>钻孔，钻透</td><td>drill a small hole<br>钻一个小孔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers drilled a small hole through the concrete wall.  工人们在混凝土墙上钻了一个小孔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 groove</td><td>/ɡruːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>享受，极其满意，过得快活</td><td>groove on the music<br>沉醉于音乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole crowd was grooving to the band's long final song.  全场观众都沉醉在乐队那首长长的终曲里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pattern</td><td>/ˈpætərn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>范例，样板；模式</td><td>a pattern to follow<br>可效法的样板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform became a pattern for other cities to follow.  这项改革成了其他城市效法的样板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 routine</td><td>/ruˈtiːn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>例行的；常规程序</td><td>a routine check<br>例行检查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The problem was discovered during a routine inspection last week.  这个问题是上周例行检查时发现的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 treadmill</td><td>/ˈtredˌmɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>枯燥单调的工作；跑步机</td><td>the daily treadmill of work<br>日复一日的枯燥工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She quit her job to escape the treadmill of endless meetings.  她辞职以逃离没完没了的会议苦役。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rationale</b></td><td>/ˌræʃəˈnæl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>基本原理，解释</td><td>the rationale behind the decision<br>决定背后的理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody explained the rationale behind the decision to close the library.  没有人解释关闭图书馆这一决定背后的道理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 account</td><td>/əˈkaʊnt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>说明，解释；账户</td><td>give a full account<br>作出完整说明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness gave a detailed account of what she had seen.  证人详细说明了她所看到的情况。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accounting</td><td>/əˈkaʊntɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>说明，解释；会计</td><td>a clear accounting of costs<br>对成本的清楚说明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board demanded a full accounting of the missing funds.  董事会要求对失踪资金作出完整说明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 argument</td><td>/ˈɑːrɡjumənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（逻辑上的）论证，理由；争论</td><td>a convincing argument<br>有说服力的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He built a convincing argument for keeping the old bridge open.  他提出了一个令人信服的论证，主张保留旧桥通行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 case</td><td>/ˈkeɪs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>情况，案例；箱子</td><td>in that case<br>那样的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In every case we studied, the same pattern appeared again.  在我们研究的每个案例中，同样的模式都再次出现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 explanation</td><td>/ˌekspləˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>解释，说明</td><td>offer a plausible explanation<br>提出合理的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His explanation for the delay satisfied nobody at the table.  他对延误的解释没有让桌上任何人满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reason</td><td>/ˈriːzən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>理由，原因；推理</td><td>a good reason for<br>…的充分理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave no reason for leaving the meeting early.  他没有给出提前离开会议的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rouse</b></td><td>/raʊz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，煽动；唤醒</td><td>rouse public anger<br>激起公众愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speech was meant to rouse public anger against the proposed tax.  那场演讲意在激起公众对拟征税款的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agitate</td><td>/ˈædʒɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>煽动，激起；使不安</td><td>agitate for reform<br>鼓动改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students agitated for changes to the exam system.  学生们为改革考试制度而奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excite</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，引发；使兴奋</td><td>excite public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech excited strong resentment among the workers.  他的演讲激起了工人们的强烈不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 motivate</td><td>/ˈmoʊtɪveɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，激发</td><td>motivate the students<br>激励学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small weekly rewards motivate the students far better than long lectures do.  每周的小奖励比长篇说教更能激励学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provoke</td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulate</td><td>/ˈstɪmjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，激发；促进</td><td>stimulate economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm rain stimulated the seeds to sprout within a week.  温暖的雨水刺激种子一周内发芽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arouse</td><td>/əˈraʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>唤起，激起；唤醒</td><td>arouse public suspicion<br>引起公众怀疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The missing files aroused suspicion among the auditors.  丢失的文件引起了审计人员的怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awake</td><td>/əˈweɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>醒着的；唤醒</td><td>lie awake at night<br>夜里醒着躺着</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The noise from the street kept me awake until dawn.  街上的噪音让我一直醒到黎明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awaken</td><td>/əˈweɪkən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>唤醒；激起</td><td>awaken old memories<br>唤起旧日记忆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of bread awakened memories of his grandmother's kitchen.  面包的香味唤起了他对祖母厨房的回忆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waken</td><td>/ˈweɪkən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>唤醒，醒来</td><td>waken the sleeping child<br>唤醒睡着的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try not to waken the baby when you come in.  你进来时尽量不要弄醒宝宝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alleviate</td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 palliate</del></td><td>/ˈpælieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓和；掩饰</td><td>palliate the symptoms<br>缓解症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new drug can only palliate the pain, not cure the disease.  这种新药只能减轻疼痛，无法治愈疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lull</td><td>/lʌl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>相对平静时期，间隙；使镇静，使安心</td><td>a lull in the fighting<br>战斗中的间隙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief lull in the fighting allowed medics to reach the wounded.  战斗中短暂的间隙让医护人员得以接近伤员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>royalty</b></td><td>/ˈrɔɪəlti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>皇家身份；版税；专利权税</td><td>a member of royalty<br>王室成员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Members of royalty attended the ceremony in full dress.  王室成员盛装出席了这场典礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aristocracy</td><td>/ˌerəˈstɑːkrəsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>贵族阶层</td><td>the landed aristocracy<br>拥有土地的贵族</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The revolution stripped the aristocracy of most of its estates.  革命剥夺了贵族大部分的地产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nobility</td><td>/noʊˈbɪləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>高贵，崇高；贵族</td><td>nobility of character<br>品格的高贵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His quiet nobility of character impressed everyone who met him.  他沉静高贵的品格令每个见过他的人印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>rubicund</b></del></td><td>/ˈruːbɪkənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>红润的，健康的</td><td>a rubicund complexion<br>红润的面色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His rubicund complexion suggested a long life spent outdoors.  他红润的气色显示他常年在户外生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blooming</td><td>/ˈbluːmɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>红润的；盛开的</td><td>blooming with health<br>气色红润健康</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She returned from the coast blooming with color and energy.  她从海边回来，脸色红润、精神饱满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 florid</td><td>/ˈflɔːrɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>辞藻华丽的，花哨的</td><td>florid prose<br>辞藻华丽的散文</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic mocked the florid prose of the young novelist.  评论家嘲讽这位年轻小说家辞藻华丽的文风。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flush</td><td>/flʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>充裕的，有钱的；冲洗</td><td>flush with money<br>钱很充裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the bonus he was flush with money for months.  拿到奖金后他有好几个月钱都很充裕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rosy</td><td>/ˈroʊzi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观的</td><td>paint a rosy picture<br>描绘乐观前景</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their report paints a rosy picture that ignores the falling export figures.  他们的报告描绘出乐观前景，却忽视了下滑的出口数据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruddy</td><td>/ˈrʌdi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>红润的，泛红的</td><td>ruddy cheeks<br>红润的双颊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer's ruddy face showed years of outdoor work.  这位农民红润的脸显示出多年的户外劳作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sanguine</td><td>/ˈsæŋɡwɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观的，确信的</td><td>sanguine about the future<br>对未来乐观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remains sanguine about the harvest despite three weeks without rain.  尽管三周无雨，她对收成依然乐观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ashen</td><td>/ˈæʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灰白的，苍白的</td><td>an ashen face<br>苍白的脸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He came back from the hospital with an ashen face.  他从医院回来时脸色灰白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pale</td><td>/ˈpeɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>苍白的；浅色的</td><td>turn pale with fear<br>吓得脸色发白</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She turned pale when she heard the bad news.  听到坏消息时她脸色发白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pallid</td><td>/ˈpælɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无生气的，缺乏活力的</td><td>a pallid imitation<br>毫无生气的模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The remake was a pallid imitation of a far livelier original.  这部翻拍片是对一部活力十足的原作的毫无生气的模仿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wan</td><td>/wɑːn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>苍白的，病态的</td><td>a wan smile<br>苍白无力的一笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave us a wan smile and went back to bed.  她苍白无力地笑了一下，又回床上躺着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rudimentary</b></td><td>/ˌruːdɪˈmentri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>初始的，未发展的；最根本的，基础的</td><td>rudimentary knowledge<br>基础的知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He had only a rudimentary knowledge of chemistry when he joined the lab.  他加入实验室时对化学只有基础的了解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incipient</td><td>/ɪnˈsɪpiənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>起初的，初现的</td><td>incipient signs of decay<br>初现的衰败迹象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dentist spotted incipient signs of decay in two of the teeth.  牙医发现两颗牙齿上有初现的蛀坏迹象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nascent</td><td>/ˈnæsnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>新生的</td><td>a nascent industry<br>新兴的行业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Government loans helped the nascent solar industry survive its first decade.  政府贷款帮助新兴的太阳能产业度过了最初十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 primitive</td><td>/ˈprɪmətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>原始的；简陋的；早期的</td><td>primitive tools<br>原始的工具</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Archaeologists uncovered primitive stone tools beside the ancient riverbed.  考古学家在古河床旁发掘出原始的石制工具。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 primordial</td><td>/praɪˈmɔːrdiəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>原始的，最初的</td><td>primordial soup<br>原始生命之汤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Life may have begun in a primordial soup of simple chemicals.  生命可能始于由简单化学物质构成的原始之汤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 basic</td><td>/ˈbeɪsɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>基本的，基础的</td><td>basic skills and knowledge<br>基本技能与知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students must master the basic rules before writing essays.  学生写文章前必须掌握基本规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elemental</td><td>/ˌeləˈmentəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>基本的，根本的</td><td>elemental human needs<br>人的基本需求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The story explores elemental fears of darkness and loss.  这个故事探讨对黑暗与失去的原始恐惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 essential</td><td>/ɪˈsenʃəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>本质的，必不可少的</td><td>an essential quality<br>本质的特性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is an essential part of her character.  耐心是她性格中本质的一部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fundamental</td><td>/ˌfʌndəˈmentəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>基本的；根本原理</td><td>a fundamental principle<br>一条根本原则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trust is fundamental to any lasting partnership.  信任对任何持久的合作都是根本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rudimental</td><td>/ˌruːdəˈmɛntəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>基础的，初步的</td><td>rudimental training<br>基础训练</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His rudimental grasp of Latin was enough to read the inscription.  他对拉丁语的初步掌握足以读懂那段铭文。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 underlying</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈlaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>根本的，潜在的</td><td>the underlying cause<br>根本原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors searched for the underlying cause of her fatigue.  医生们寻找她疲倦的根本原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 developed</td><td>/dɪˈveləpt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>发达的，成熟的</td><td>a highly developed economy<br>高度发达的经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The island became a highly developed economy within one generation.  这个岛在一代人之内成为高度发达的经济体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 full-blown</td><td>/ˌfʊlˈbloʊn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全成熟的，全面爆发的</td><td>a full-blown crisis<br>全面爆发的危机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A minor dispute grew into a full-blown lawsuit.  一场小争执演变成全面的诉讼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mature</td><td>/məˈtʃʊr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>成熟的；（使）成熟</td><td>a mature judgment<br>成熟的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He seems remarkably mature for a boy of fifteen.  对一个十五岁男孩来说他显得格外成熟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ripe</td><td>/ˈraɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>成熟的，熟透的；时机成熟的</td><td>ripe for change<br>变革时机成熟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaches are finally ripe enough to pick and eat.  这些桃子终于熟到可以采下来吃了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 advanced</td><td>/ədˈvænst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>先进的，高级的</td><td>advanced technology<br>先进的技术</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab uses advanced technology unavailable elsewhere in the country.  该实验室使用国内其他地方没有的先进技术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 higher</td><td>/ˈhaɪər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>更高级的，更高的</td><td>higher education<br>高等教育</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These courses demand higher reasoning than introductory ones.  这些课程要求比入门课更高层次的推理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rue</b></td><td>/ruː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>后悔，遗憾</td><td>to his rue<br>令他后悔的是</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 To his lasting rue, he sold the painting for almost nothing.  令他长久后悔的是，他几乎白送掉了那幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 contriteness</del></td><td>/kənˈtraɪtnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>悔悟，痛悔</td><td>genuine contriteness<br>真诚的悔悟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letter's contriteness persuaded the judge to reduce his sentence.  信中的悔意说服法官减轻了他的刑罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contrition</td><td>/kənˈtrɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>痛悔，悔罪</td><td>an act of contrition<br>一次悔罪之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spoke with such contrition that his friends forgave him.  他说话时那样痛悔，朋友们都原谅了他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 penitence</td><td>/ˈpɛnɪtəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>悔悟，忏悔</td><td>a sign of penitence<br>悔悟的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of quiet penitence followed that one reckless night.  那一个鲁莽的夜晚之后是多年的默默忏悔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regret</td><td>/rəˈɡret/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>后悔，遗憾；懊悔</td><td>regret his decision<br>后悔他的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She regretted not saying goodbye before he left the country.  她后悔在他出国前没有道别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remorse</td><td>/rɪˈmɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>懊悔，悔恨</td><td>show no remorse<br>毫无悔意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defendant showed no remorse for the harm he had caused.  被告对自己造成的伤害毫无悔意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 remorsefulness</del></td><td>/rɪˈmɔrsfəlnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>悔恨，懊悔</td><td>deep remorsefulness<br>深深的悔恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her remorsefulness was obvious in every line of the apology.  她的悔恨在道歉信的每一行里都显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repentance</td><td>/rɪˈpentəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>悔悟，忏悔</td><td>sincere repentance<br>真诚的悔悟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The parole board looked for evidence of genuine repentance.  假释委员会在寻找真正悔悟的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 impenitence</del></td><td>/ɪmˈpɛnɪtəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不悔悟，顽固不改</td><td>stubborn impenitence<br>顽固的不悔悟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court noted his impenitence when deciding the sentence.  法庭在量刑时注意到他毫无悔意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 remorselessness</del></td><td>/rɪˈmɔrsləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无悔意，冷酷无情</td><td>chilling remorselessness<br>令人心寒的冷酷无情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Witnesses were shaken by the remorselessness in his voice.  证人们被他语气中的冷酷无情惊住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ruffle</b></td><td>/ˈrʌfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>弄乱（毛发）；扰乱，使恼怒</td><td>ruffle his feathers<br>惹他不快</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single rude question was enough to ruffle the normally calm witness.  一个无礼的问题就足以扰乱这位平素冷静的证人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abrade</td><td>/əˈbreɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>磨损，擦伤；使烦躁</td><td>abrade the skin<br>擦伤皮肤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rough rope abraded the sailor's palms.  粗糙的绳子磨伤了水手的手掌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chafe</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪf/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>摩擦致痛，磨破；恼怒</td><td>chafe against the collar<br>衣领磨得发痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff new boots chafed his heels until they bled.  硬邦邦的新靴子把他的脚跟磨出了血。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 erode</td><td>/ɪˈroʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>侵蚀，磨损</td><td>erode the coastline<br>侵蚀海岸线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rains slowly erode the exposed hillside each spring.  每年春天大雨慢慢侵蚀裸露的山坡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 roughen</del></td><td>/ˈrʌfən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使变粗糙</td><td>roughen the surface<br>使表面变粗糙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sand the board to roughen it before applying the glue.  上胶前用砂纸把木板打磨粗糙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rub</td><td>/ˈrʌb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>摩擦，擦</td><td>rub against the grain<br>逆着纹理摩擦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not rub your eyes with dirty hands.  不要用脏手揉眼睛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wear</td><td>/ˈwer/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忍受，容忍；穿戴</td><td>will not wear that excuse<br>不会容忍那种借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager would not wear such a feeble excuse again.  经理不会再容忍这种苍白的借口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 annoy</td><td>/əˈnɔɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒，不断烦扰</td><td>annoy the neighbors<br>不断打扰邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The barking dog began to annoy every neighbor on the quiet street.  那只不停犬吠的狗开始烦扰这条安静街上的每位邻居。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bother</td><td>/ˈbɑːðər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>打扰，使烦恼；费心</td><td>don't bother me<br>别烦我</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constant noise from the street really bothers me.  街上不停的噪音真让我烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frost</td><td>/ˈfrɔːst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使恼怒；冷淡对待</td><td>frost the whole audience<br>把全场观众惹恼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His smug reply frosted everyone in the small room.  他自得的回答把小屋里的人都惹恼了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gall</td><td>/ɡɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（使）焦躁，激怒</td><td>galled by the remark<br>被那句话惹恼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His smug tone galled everyone sitting around the conference table.  他自鸣得意的语气激怒了围坐在会议桌旁的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grate</td><td>/ɡreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>刮擦（以发出刺耳的声音）；骚扰，惹恼</td><td>grate on my nerves<br>让我心烦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her constant humming began to grate on everyone in the small office.  她不停的哼唱开始让小办公室里的每个人心烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 itch</td><td>/ˈɪtʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>渴望；发痒</td><td>itch to travel<br>渴望去旅行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were itching to open their presents early.  孩子们急着想早点拆礼物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nettle</td><td>/ˈnetl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>惹怒</td><td>nettled by criticism<br>被批评惹怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was clearly nettled by the reporter's rude question.  记者无礼的提问显然惹恼了她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peeve</td><td>/piːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打扰，惹恼</td><td>peeve the audience<br>惹恼观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud phone conversations on the train really peeve the other passengers.  火车上大声打电话确实惹恼其他乘客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 persecute</td><td>/ˈpɜːrsɪkjuːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>迫害，折磨</td><td>persecute religious minorities<br>迫害宗教少数群体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime persecuted religious minorities for more than a decade.  该政权迫害宗教少数群体十多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pique</td><td>/piːk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起（兴趣）；使生气</td><td>pique her curiosity<br>激起她的好奇心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strange title piqued her curiosity, so she borrowed the book.  奇怪的书名激起了她的好奇心，于是她借走了那本书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rasp</td><td>/ˈræsp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>用粗锉磨；沙哑地说</td><td>rasp a rough edge<br>锉平粗糙的边缘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rasped the wood smooth before painting it.  他把木头锉平后再上漆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rile</td><td>/raɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，惹怒</td><td>rile up the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing riles him more than being interrupted in the middle of a sentence.  没有什么比话说到一半被打断更让他恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vex</td><td>/veks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使烦恼，使恼怒</td><td>vex the teacher<br>使老师恼怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant interruptions vexed the teacher throughout the long afternoon lesson.  不断的打扰让老师在漫长的下午课上十分恼怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glaze</td><td>/ɡleɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给…上釉，妆点</td><td>glaze the pottery<br>给陶器上釉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The artisan will glaze each bowl before firing it a second time.  匠人会在第二次烧制前给每只碗上釉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 smooth</td><td>/ˈsmuːð/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>温和的，圆滑的；平滑的</td><td>a smooth talker<br>说话圆滑的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a smooth talker who never offends anyone in negotiations.  他谈判时言辞圆滑，从不得罪任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alleviate</td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 palliate</del></td><td>/ˈpælieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓和；掩饰</td><td>palliate the symptoms<br>缓解症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new drug can only palliate the pain, not cure the disease.  这种新药只能减轻疼痛，无法治愈疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ruminate</b></td><td>/ˈruːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>沉思；反复琢磨；反刍</td><td>ruminate on the past<br>反复思量往事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and ruminated on the past.  他坐在窗边，反复回想往事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cogitate</del></td><td>/ˈkɑːdʒɪˌteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>认真思考，仔细考虑</td><td>cogitate on the problem<br>仔细思考这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and cogitated on his next move.  他坐在窗边，仔细思量下一步该怎么走。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contemplate</td><td>/ˈkɑːntəmpleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>沉思，仔细思索</td><td>contemplate the consequences<br>仔细思索后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sat by the window and contemplated the few choices left to her.  她坐在窗边，仔细思索留给自己的几个选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deliberate</td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meditate</td><td>/ˈmedɪteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>思索，沉思</td><td>meditate on the problem<br>沉思这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and meditated on the day's events.  他坐在窗边沉思白天发生的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 perpend</del></td><td>/pərˈpɛnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>仔细考虑，斟酌</td><td>perpend the evidence<br>仔细斟酌证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge asked the jury to perpend the evidence overnight.  法官要陪审团用一夜时间仔细斟酌证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ponder</td><td>/ˈpɑːndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>仔细考虑，深思</td><td>ponder the question<br>细想这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He pondered the offer for a week before finally accepting it.  他把这个提议考虑了一周才最终接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weigh</td><td>/ˈweɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>权衡，仔细考虑</td><td>weigh the risks<br>权衡各种风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They weighed the risks for several hours before agreeing.  他们权衡风险数小时后才达成一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignore</td><td>/ˌɪɡˈnɔːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视，不理</td><td>ignore the warning<br>忽视警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain ignored the warning and sailed into the storm.  船长忽视了警告，驶入了风暴之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 neglect</td><td>/nəˈɡlekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>忽视，疏忽；忽略，怠慢</td><td>neglect his duties<br>疏忽职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He neglected his duties for weeks and was finally dismissed.  他数周疏忽职责，最终被解雇了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 overlook</td><td>/ˈoʊvərˌlʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视；宽恕；俯视</td><td>overlook a minor error<br>宽容小错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher chose to overlook the boy's late arrival.  老师决定不追究那男孩的迟到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rumple</b></td><td>/ˈrʌmpl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打乱，使不整齐</td><td>rumple his hair<br>把他头发弄乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His aunt always rumpled the little boy's hair when she visited.  他姨妈每次来都会揉乱小男孩的头发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disarray</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈreɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>混乱，无秩序</td><td>in complete disarray<br>一片混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The office was in complete disarray after the movers left boxes everywhere.  搬运工把箱子扔得到处都是，办公室一片混乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disorder</td><td>/dɪˈsɔːrdər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>混乱，无序；失调，疾病</td><td>public disorder<br>公共秩序混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was left in complete disorder after the party.  聚会之后房间被弄得一片混乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disorganize</del></td><td>/dɪˈsɔːrɡəˌnaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使混乱，扰乱</td><td>disorganize the schedule<br>打乱日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The storm disorganized every flight leaving the airport that day.  风暴打乱了那天所有离港航班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disrupt</td><td>/dɪsˈrʌpt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扰乱，打乱</td><td>disrupt the meeting<br>扰乱会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Protesters disrupted the ceremony by shouting from the balcony.  抗议者在楼上喊叫，扰乱了典礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disturb</td><td>/dɪˈstɜːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打扰，扰乱；使不安</td><td>disturb the peace<br>扰乱安宁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please do not disturb the birds while they are nesting.  鸟类筑巢时请不要打扰它们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mess</td><td>/ˈmes/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>混乱，脏乱；弄乱</td><td>make a mess of it<br>把它弄得一团糟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen was a terrible mess when the children finished baking.  孩子们烤完点心后厨房一片狼藉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muddle</td><td>/ˈmʌdəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使混乱，弄糟；混乱</td><td>muddle the facts<br>把事实弄乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many footnotes muddled the argument of his short essay.  过多脚注把他短文的论证弄乱了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upset</td><td>/əpˈset/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使心烦，使不安；打乱</td><td>upset the schedule<br>打乱日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of the closure upset the staff more than management expected.  停业的消息比管理层预想的更让员工不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 arrange</td><td>/əˈreɪndʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>安排，筹划；整理</td><td>arrange the meeting<br>安排会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She arranged the whole trip in a single busy afternoon.  她在一个忙碌的下午就安排好了整趟行程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 array</td><td>/əˈreɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一批，一系列；排列</td><td>an impressive array<br>蔚为壮观的一批</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum displayed an impressive array of ancient coins.  博物馆展出了一批蔚为壮观的古钱币。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 order</td><td>/ˈɔːrdər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>秩序，条理；命令</td><td>restore public order<br>恢复公共秩序</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police worked all night to restore order in the city.  警察通宵工作以恢复城市的秩序。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 organize</td><td>/ˈɔːrɡəˌnaɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>组织，筹划，安排</td><td>organize the conference<br>筹办会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three students organized the conference without any help from staff.  三名学生在没有职工帮助下筹办了这次会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regulate</td><td>/ˈreɡjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>管理，调控，规范</td><td>regulate the industry<br>对该行业进行监管</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New laws regulate how companies collect personal data.  新法规范了公司收集个人数据的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>revision</b></td><td>/rɪˈvɪʒn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>修订</td><td>make a final revision<br>做最后一次修订</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author made one final revision before sending the manuscript to press.  作者在把书稿送去付印前做了最后一次修订。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alteration</td><td>/ˌɔːltəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>改动，变更</td><td>minor alterations to the plan<br>对计划的小改动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tailor made a small alteration to the sleeve length.  裁缝对袖长做了小小改动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 modification</td><td>/ˌmɑːdəfəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>修改，改进</td><td>slight modification of design<br>设计的略微修改</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers approved one modification to the engine housing.  工程师批准了对发动机外壳的一处改进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remodeling</td><td>/rɪˈmɑːdəlɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>改建，改造</td><td>kitchen remodeling project<br>厨房改造工程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The remodeling of the old library took eighteen months.  旧图书馆的改建花了十八个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revise</td><td>/rɪˈvaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>修改，修订</td><td>revise the draft carefully<br>仔细修改草稿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had to revise the report after new data arrived.  新数据到来后她不得不修改报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 change</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪndʒ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>改变，变化；零钱</td><td>change your mind<br>改变主意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing anyone said could change his mind about leaving.  谁说什么都无法改变他离开的主意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 variation</td><td>/ˌveriˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>变化，变体</td><td>slight variation in color<br>颜色的细微变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is wide variation in rainfall across the region.  该地区降雨量差异很大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fixation</td><td>/fɪkˈseɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>固定；固着，执念</td><td>the fixation of nitrogen<br>氮的固定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treatment relies on the fixation of the broken bone.  这种治疗依赖于对断骨的固定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stabilization</td><td>/ˌsteɪbələˈzeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>稳定，稳固</td><td>price stabilization measures<br>价格稳定措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stabilization of the currency reassured foreign investors.  货币的稳定让外国投资者安心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>runic</b></td><td>/ˈruːnɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>神秘的</td><td>runic symbols and marks<br>神秘的符号与标记</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strange runic markings covered the stone we found beside the river.  我们在河边发现的石头上布满神秘的符号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arcane</td><td>/ɑːrˈkeɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深奥的，难以理解的</td><td>arcane rules<br>晦涩难懂的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax code is full of arcane rules nobody understands.  税法充满无人理解的晦涩规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enigmatic</td><td>/ˌenɪɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>神秘的，难以理解的</td><td>an enigmatic smile<br>神秘的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her enigmatic answer left the reporters guessing for several days.  她神秘的回答让记者们猜了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impenetrable</td><td>/ɪmˈpenɪtrəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>impenetrable jargon<br>晦涩难懂的行话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract was written in impenetrable jargon that confused everyone.  合同用晦涩难懂的行话写成，让所有人都困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inscrutable</td><td>/ɪnˈskruːtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高深莫测的，难以理解的</td><td>an inscrutable expression<br>高深莫测的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His inscrutable smile gave nothing away about his plans.  他那高深莫测的笑容丝毫没有透露他的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mysterious</td><td>/mɪˈstɪriəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>神秘的，难以理解的</td><td>a mysterious stranger<br>神秘的陌生人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A mysterious light appeared over the lake around midnight.  午夜时分湖面上出现了一道神秘的光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mystic</td><td>/ˈmɪstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>神秘的，玄妙的</td><td>a mystic experience<br>神秘的体验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old poem describes a mystic union with the natural world.  这首古诗描写了与自然界的神秘合一。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occult</td><td>/əˈkʌlt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>occult knowledge<br>玄奥的知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manuscript is filled with occult knowledge that scholars still cannot decipher.  这份手稿满是学者至今无法解读的玄奥知识。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncanny</td><td>/ʌnˈkæni/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>离奇的，奇异的</td><td>an uncanny resemblance<br>异乎寻常的相似</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The twins share an uncanny ability to finish each other's sentences.  这对双胞胎有一种离奇的本事，能接上对方的话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 comprehensible</td><td>/ˌkɑːmpriˈhensəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的，易懂的</td><td>clear and comprehensible<br>清晰易懂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers make difficult ideas comprehensible to complete beginners.  好老师能让初学者也理解艰深的概念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 fathomable</del></td><td>/ˈfæðəməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的，可探明的</td><td>hardly fathomable motives<br>难以捉摸的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her reasons for leaving were not easily fathomable to us.  她离开的原因我们不容易理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rupture</b></td><td>/ˈrʌptʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>打破；打碎，破裂</td><td>rupture a blood vessel<br>使血管破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old pipe could rupture at any moment under such high pressure.  这根旧管道随时可能在如此高的压力下破裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breach</td><td>/briːtʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>违背</td><td>breach the contract<br>违约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company breached the contract by delivering the goods late.  这家公司延迟交货，违反了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burst</td><td>/ˈbɜːrst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>爆裂；突发，爆发</td><td>burst into flames<br>突然燃起大火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old pipe burst and flooded the basement within minutes.  旧管子爆裂，几分钟就淹了地下室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disjoin</del></td><td>/dɪsˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分开，使分离</td><td>disjoin the two parts<br>把两部分分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer had to disjoin the pipes to replace a valve.  工程师得把管子分开才能更换阀门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissect</td><td>/dɪˈsekt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解剖；仔细分析</td><td>dissect the argument<br>仔细剖析该论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics dissected the argument line by line and exposed its hidden assumptions.  批评者逐句剖析该论点，揭出其隐含的假设。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fracture</td><td>/ˈfræktʃər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>破裂</td><td>a hairline fracture<br>一道细微裂缝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scan revealed a hairline fracture in his left wrist.  扫描显示他左手腕有一道细微裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 split</td><td>/ˈsplɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分裂，分开</td><td>split into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee split into two camps over the proposal.  委员会因该提案分裂成两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sunder</td><td>/ˈsʌndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，割断</td><td>sunder the alliance<br>使联盟破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bitter dispute sundered an alliance that had lasted decades.  这场激烈的争端割断了维持数十年的联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tear</td><td>/ˈter/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>撕开，扯破；眼泪</td><td>tear the paper in half<br>把纸撕成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tore the photograph in half and threw it away.  她把照片撕成两半扔掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 associate</td><td>/əˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，结交；联想</td><td>associate with the group<br>与该团体结交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to associate himself with such a risky plan.  他拒绝与这样冒险的计划有牵连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coalesce</td><td>/ˌkoʊəˈles/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>合并，融合</td><td>coalesce into a group<br>合并成一个团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scattered protests coalesced into a national movement.  分散的抗议汇合成一场全国性运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 combine</td><td>/kəmˈbaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，联合</td><td>combine two elements<br>结合两种元素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The recipe combines sweet and sour flavors very successfully.  这道菜谱把甜味和酸味结合得很成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conjoin</td><td>/kənˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，连接</td><td>conjoin the two parts<br>把两部分接合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge conjoins the island and the mainland neatly.  这座桥把小岛与大陆巧妙相连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conjugate</td><td>/ˈkɑːndʒəɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>结合，配对；成对的</td><td>conjugate two molecules<br>使两个分子结合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers conjugate the drug to an antibody for precise delivery.  研究者把药物与抗体结合以实现精准投递。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 connect</td><td>/kəˈnekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，联结</td><td>connect the two wires<br>连接两根导线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow tunnel connects the two halves of the building.  一条窄隧道把大楼的两半连起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fuse</td><td>/ˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，熔合</td><td>fuse two traditions<br>融合两种传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her music fuses jazz rhythms with traditional folk melodies.  她的音乐把爵士节奏与传统民谣旋律融为一体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 interfuse</del></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈfjuz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使混合，交融</td><td>interfuse the two styles<br>交融两种风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The composer interfuses folk melodies with modern harmony.  作曲家把民间旋律与现代和声交融在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unify</td><td>/ˈjuːnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>统一，使成一体</td><td>unify the country<br>统一国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A common language helped unify the scattered tribes into one nation.  共同的语言帮助把分散的部落统一为一个民族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ruse</b></td><td>/ruːz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计</td><td>a clever ruse<br>一个巧妙的诡计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empty truck was a clever ruse to draw the guards away.  那辆空卡车是个巧妙的诡计，用来引开守卫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 artifice</td><td>/ˈɑːrtəfɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计，欺骗；巧妙手段</td><td>use clever artifice<br>使用巧妙的诡计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her apparent shyness was pure artifice designed to disarm her rivals.  她表面的羞怯纯属伪装，为的是麻痹对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maneuver</td><td>/məˈnuːvər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>巧妙地操纵</td><td>maneuver the car into place<br>把车巧妙地挪到位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She patiently maneuvered the car into a very narrow parking space.  她耐心地把车挪进一个非常狭窄的停车位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stratagem</td><td>/ˈstrætədʒəm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>谋略，策略</td><td>a military stratagem<br>一项军事谋略</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general won the pass by a simple stratagem.  将军用一个简单的谋略夺取了关隘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trick</td><td>/ˈtrɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>欺骗，诱骗；诡计</td><td>trick someone into paying<br>诱骗某人付钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tricked him into revealing the password to the account.  她骗他说出了账户密码。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wile</td><td>/ˈwaɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计，花招</td><td>use every wile<br>使尽各种花招</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She used every wile she knew to win his support.  她用尽一切手段来赢得他的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rustic</b></td><td>/ˈrʌstɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>乡下人，头脑简单的人；粗俗的</td><td>rustic manners<br>粗俗的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His rustic manners amused the guests at the elegant city dinner.  他粗俗的举止让城里雅致晚宴上的客人觉得好笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bumpkin</td><td>/ˈbʌmpkɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>乡下人，土包子</td><td>a country bumpkin<br>乡下土包子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In the city he felt like a bumpkin staring at the towers.  在城里他觉得自己像个盯着高楼看的乡下人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 churl</td><td>/tʃɜrl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>粗鄙的人，乡下佬</td><td>behave like a churl<br>举止像个粗鄙之人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a churl would refuse to thank his host.  只有粗鄙之人才会不感谢主人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 countryman</td><td>/ˈkʌntrimən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>乡下人；同乡</td><td>a plain countryman<br>朴实的乡下人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old countryman knew every path through the woods.  这位老乡下人熟悉林中每条小路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provincial</td><td>/prəˈvɪnʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>狭隘（的）</td><td>provincial tastes<br>狭隘的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His provincial tastes made him suspicious of anything foreign or unfamiliar.  他狭隘的品味使他对一切外来或陌生的事物都怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rube</td><td>/ˈruːb/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>乡巴佬，土气的人</td><td>treated like a rube<br>被当作乡巴佬对待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The salesman took him for a rube and raised the price.  推销员把他当乡巴佬，抬高了价钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yokel</td><td>/ˈjoʊkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>乡下人；土包子</td><td>a gawking yokel<br>呆看的乡下人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stood in the marble lobby like a gawking country yokel.  他站在大理石大堂里，像个东张西望的乡下土包子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bucolic</td><td>/bjuˈkɑːlɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>乡村的，田园的</td><td>a bucolic landscape<br>田园风光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent the summer in a bucolic village far from traffic.  他们在远离车流的田园村庄度过了夏天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 country</td><td>/ˈkʌntri/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>乡下的，乡村的</td><td>a quiet country road<br>安静的乡间小路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We drove along a narrow country lane lined with hedges.  我们沿着两旁有树篱的乡间小路开车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pastoral</td><td>/ˈpæstərəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>田园的，牧歌式的</td><td>a pastoral scene<br>田园牧歌的景象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painter is famous for pastoral scenes of shepherds and hills.  这位画家以牧羊人与山丘的田园画作闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clumsy</td><td>/ˈklʌmzi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不灵巧的</td><td>a clumsy attempt<br>笨拙的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My clumsy attempt at juggling ended with three broken plates.  我笨拙地学抛球，结果打碎了三个盘子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discourteous</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrtiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，粗鲁的</td><td>a discourteous reply<br>无礼的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was discourteous to leave without thanking the host.  不向主人道谢就离开是无礼的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gauche</td><td>/ɡoʊʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，缺乏社交经验的</td><td>a gauche remark<br>笨拙失礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His gauche remark about her salary embarrassed the whole table.  他关于她薪水的笨拙失礼的话让整桌人都很尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impertinent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrtnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相关的；无礼的，鲁莽的</td><td>an impertinent remark<br>无礼的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer objected that the question was impertinent to the charges.  律师反对说，这个问题与指控无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impolite</td><td>/ˌɪmpəˈlaɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，粗鲁的</td><td>an impolite remark<br>无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is impolite to interrupt someone in the middle of a sentence.  在别人话没说完时插嘴很不礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inelegant</td><td>/ɪnˈeləɡənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不雅的，粗俗的</td><td>an inelegant solution<br>不够优雅的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His inelegant table manners embarrassed the whole family.  他不雅的餐桌举止让全家都很尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rude</td><td>/ˈruːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的，无礼的</td><td>a rude remark<br>无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was rude of him to leave without thanking the host.  他不谢主人就离开，很没礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stiff</td><td>/stɪf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵硬的，无法弯曲的；艰苦的，费力的</td><td>a stiff leather collar<br>硬挺的皮质衣领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff leather collar rubbed his neck raw during the long march.  长途行军中，硬挺的皮领把他的脖子磨破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cosmopolitan</td><td>/ˌkɑːzməˈpɑːlɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有世界性眼光的，包容的</td><td>a cosmopolitan city<br>国际化的都市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years abroad gave her a cosmopolitan outlook her old classmates clearly lacked.  多年国外生活给了她老同学明显缺乏的世界性眼光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 cosmopolite</del></td><td>/kɑzˈmɑpəlaɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>见识广的世界主义者</td><td>a true cosmopolite<br>真正的世界通</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cosmopolite by nature, she felt at home in any capital.  她天性四海为家，在任何首都都感到自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sophisticate</td><td>/səˈfɪstəˌkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使复杂化；老于世故者</td><td>sophisticate the design further<br>把设计进一步复杂化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers sophisticated the model until nobody could explain it.  工程师把模型改得越来越复杂，最后没人能解释清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 graceful</td><td>/ˈɡreɪsfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>优雅的，优美的</td><td>a graceful dancer<br>姿态优雅的舞者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a graceful turn and landed without a sound.  她优雅地一个转身，落地时毫无声响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 polished</td><td>/ˈpɑːlɪʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的</td><td>polished manners<br>优雅得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His polished manners impressed everyone at the formal dinner.  他优雅得体的举止让正式晚宴上的每个人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 urbane</td><td>/ərˈbeɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>彬彬有礼的，文雅的</td><td>an urbane host<br>文雅的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our urbane host guided the conversation with easy wit and grace.  我们文雅的主人以轻松的机智和风度引导着谈话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rustle</b></td><td>/ˈrʌsl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>快速地行动</td><td>rustle up a meal<br>快手弄出一顿饭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She rustled up a hot meal for six guests in twenty minutes.  她二十分钟内为六位客人麻利地做出一顿热饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bolt</td><td>/ˈboʊlt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>突然说出；猛冲</td><td>bolt out an answer<br>脱口说出答案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He bolted out the secret before anyone could stop him.  他还没等人阻止就把秘密说了出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breeze</td><td>/ˈbriːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻而易举的事；微风</td><td>a breeze to finish<br>轻松就能做完</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final exam was a breeze compared with the weekly quizzes.  与每周的小测相比，期末考试轻松得多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 careen</td><td>/kəˈriːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇晃疾行，横冲直撞</td><td>careen down the hill<br>摇晃着冲下山坡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empty cart careened down the hill and struck a fence.  空车摇晃着冲下山坡，撞上了篱笆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 career</td><td>/kəˈrɪr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>疾驰，飞奔</td><td>career down the hill<br>沿山坡疾驰而下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empty cart careered down the slope and hit a fence.  空车沿坡疾冲而下撞上了栅栏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hasten</td><td>/ˈheɪsn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加速，促进；催促</td><td>hasten the decision<br>促使尽快决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm weather hastened the melting of snow on the high mountain passes.  暖和的天气促进了高山山口积雪的融化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hustle</td><td>/ˈhʌsəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>催促；奋力行进</td><td>hustle through the crowd<br>在人群中挤行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hustled the children into the car before the rain.  她在下雨前把孩子们匆匆赶上车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jump</td><td>/ˈdʒʌmp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>跳跃；猛冲</td><td>jump to attention<br>猛然起立注意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He jumped from his chair when the alarm sounded.  警报响起时他从椅子上一跃而起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 run</td><td>/rʌn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>跑，奔跑；运转</td><td>run for the bus<br>跑着赶公交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ran three miles before breakfast every single morning.  他每天早餐前都跑三英里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rush</td><td>/rʌʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>匆忙，急速行动；催促</td><td>rush to the airport<br>赶往机场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They rushed to the airport and barely caught the flight.  他们赶往机场，勉强赶上了航班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scurry</td><td>/ˈskɜːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>急匆匆地跑</td><td>scurry for cover<br>急忙找掩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mice scurried across the floor when the light came on.  灯一亮，老鼠们急匆匆跑过地板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 crawl</td><td>/ˈkrɔːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>缓慢行进；爬行</td><td>crawl along the highway<br>在公路上缓慢行进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic crawled along the flooded highway for nearly three hours.  车流在被淹的公路上缓慢行进了近三个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 creep</td><td>/kriːp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>缓慢地行进</td><td>creep along slowly<br>缓慢地爬行前进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic crept forward only a few meters at a time all morning.  整个上午车流每次只缓慢前进几米。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sabotage</b></td><td>/ˈsæbətɑːʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>妨害，破坏</td><td>an act of sabotage<br>一次破坏行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investigators concluded that the fire was an act of deliberate sabotage.  调查人员认定这场火灾是一次故意的破坏行动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 damage</td><td>/ˈdæmədʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>损害，破坏；损失</td><td>damage the crops<br>损害庄稼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Late frost damaged the crops across the entire river valley.  晚霜损害了整个河谷的庄稼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impairment</td><td>/ɪmˈpermənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>损害，损伤</td><td>hearing impairment<br>听力损伤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long exposure to noise caused permanent impairment of his hearing.  长期暴露在噪音中造成他听力永久损伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subversion</td><td>/səbˈvɜːrʒən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>颠覆，破坏</td><td>subversion of authority<br>对权威的颠覆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime accused the writers of subversion of public order.  当局指控这些作家颠覆公共秩序。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undermining</td><td>/ˈʌndərˌmaɪnɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>削弱，暗中破坏</td><td>the undermining of trust<br>对信任的暗中破坏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant criticism led to the undermining of her confidence.  不断的批评削弱了她的自信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disrupt</td><td>/dɪsˈrʌpt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扰乱，打乱</td><td>disrupt the meeting<br>扰乱会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Protesters disrupted the ceremony by shouting from the balcony.  抗议者在楼上喊叫，扰乱了典礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
