<tr><td>↳近 foil</td><td>/fɔɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>挫败</td><td>foil a plot<br>挫败阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Alert guards foiled the plot before anyone was hurt.  警觉的警卫在有人受伤之前挫败了阴谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frustrate</td><td>/ˈfrʌstreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻挠，使受挫；使沮丧</td><td>frustrate one's plans<br>使计划受挫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow frustrated our plans to drive home.  大雪打乱了我们开车回家的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstruct</td><td>/əbˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻塞</td><td>obstruct justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fallen tree obstructed the only road out of town.  一棵倒下的树堵住了唯一的出城道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undermine</td><td>/ˈʌndərˌmaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>削弱，暗中破坏</td><td>undermine confidence<br>削弱信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant rumors undermined confidence in the young manager.  不断的谣言削弱了人们对这位年轻经理的信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assistance</td><td>/əˈsɪstəns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>帮助，援助</td><td>offer financial assistance<br>提供财务援助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without her assistance the project would have collapsed.  没有她的帮助，这个项目早就垮了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>saccharine</b></td><td>/ˈsækərɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>甜得发腻的；矫情做作的</td><td>saccharine sentimentality<br>矫情做作的伤感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film's ending is far too saccharine for my taste.  这部电影的结局对我来说太过矫情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cloying</td><td>/ˈklɔɪɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>甜得发腻的；感情用事的</td><td>a cloying sweetness<br>甜得发腻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cake had a cloying sweetness that ruined the meal.  蛋糕甜得发腻，毁了这顿饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sugary</td><td>/ˈʃʊɡəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含糖的；甜得发腻的</td><td>sugary drinks<br>含糖饮料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The song's sugary lyrics made the critics groan.  这首歌甜得发腻的歌词让评论家们直摇头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fruity</td><td>/ˈfruːti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>果味的；甜腻的</td><td>a fruity aroma<br>果香气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wine has a fruity flavor with a hint of spice.  这款酒带果味，还有一丝辛香。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maudlin</td><td>/ˈmɔːdlɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于感伤的</td><td>maudlin sentimentality<br>过度的感伤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After two drinks he grew maudlin about his lost youth.  喝了两杯后他为逝去的青春伤感起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mawkish</td><td>/ˈmɔːkɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度伤感的</td><td>a mawkish film<br>滥情的电影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film's mawkish ending made several people in the audience laugh out loud.  这部电影滥情的结局让观众里好几个人笑出声来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mushy</td><td>/ˈmʌʃi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多愁善感的，肉麻的；软糊的</td><td>a mushy love letter<br>肉麻的情书</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wrote her a mushy letter that he later regretted.  他给她写了一封后来觉得肉麻的信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sentimental</td><td>/ˌsentəˈmentəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多情的，感情用事的</td><td>sentimental value<br>情感价值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old watch has great sentimental value for my mother.  这块旧表对我母亲有很大的情感价值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sugarcoated</td><td>/ˈʃʊɡərkoʊtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>糖衣的；粉饰美化的</td><td>a sugarcoated version<br>粉饰过的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a sugarcoated account of a very ugly quarrel.  他把一场很难看的争吵说得轻描淡写。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bitter</td><td>/ˈbɪtər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严寒刺骨的；苦的</td><td>a bitter north wind<br>刺骨的北风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We waited an hour in bitter cold for the train.  我们在刺骨的严寒中等了一小时火车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unsentimental</td><td>/ˌʌnsentɪˈmentəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感情用事的，冷静的</td><td>an unsentimental account<br>不带感情的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unsentimental account of the war impressed the historians.  她对战争不带感情的叙述令史学家印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sacrilege</b></td><td>/ˈsækrəlɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>亵渎圣物</td><td>commit an act of sacrilege<br>做出亵渎神圣之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Selling the temple bells struck the villagers as outright sacrilege.  卖掉庙里的钟在村民看来是彻底的亵渎神圣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blasphemy</td><td>/ˈblæsfəmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>渎神，亵渎的言辞</td><td>accused of blasphemy<br>被指控渎神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In that century such a remark counted as blasphemy.  在那个世纪，这样一句话算是渎神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defilement</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪlmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>污损，玷污</td><td>defilement of a temple<br>对神庙的玷污</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers mourned the defilement of the ancient shrine.  村民为古老神殿被玷污而悲痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 desecration</td><td>/ˌdesɪˈkreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>亵渎，污损圣物</td><td>desecration of graves<br>对坟墓的亵渎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The desecration of the cemetery shocked the whole town.  墓地遭亵渎震惊了全镇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irreverence</td><td>/ɪˈrevərəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不敬，无礼</td><td>cheerful irreverence<br>轻松的不恭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His irreverence toward tradition shocked the older members of the club.  他对传统的不敬震惊了俱乐部的老成员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impiety</td><td>/ɪmˈpaɪəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不敬，不虔诚</td><td>an act of impiety<br>不敬之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The philosopher was tried for impiety toward the city's gods.  这位哲学家因对城邦神灵不敬而受审。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profanation</td><td>/ˌprɑːfəˈneɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>亵渎，滥用圣物</td><td>profanation of sacred texts<br>对圣典的亵渎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Using the relic as decoration struck them as profanation.  把圣物当装饰在他们看来是亵渎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 violation</td><td>/vaɪəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>违反，侵犯</td><td>a violation of the rules<br>违反规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parking here is a direct violation of the city ordinance.  在此停车直接违反了市政条例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adoration</td><td>/ˌædəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>崇敬，爱慕</td><td>gaze with adoration<br>以崇敬的目光注视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd looked at the old singer with pure adoration.  人群以纯粹的爱慕注视着这位老歌手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glorification</td><td>/ˌɡlɔːrəfɪˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赞美，颂扬</td><td>glorification of war<br>对战争的颂扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics attacked the film for its glorification of violence.  评论家批评这部电影美化暴力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 respect</td><td>/rɪˈspekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>尊敬，敬重；方面</td><td>show respect for<br>对……表示尊敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students should learn to respect opinions that differ from their own.  学生应学会尊重与自己不同的意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reverence</td><td>/ˈrevərəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>崇敬，尊崇</td><td>deep reverence for nature<br>对自然的深切崇敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old scholar is treated with reverence by his many students.  这位老学者受到众多学生的崇敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>saddle</b></td><td>/ˈsædl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使某人负担</td><td>saddle him with blame<br>让他背上责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new rules saddle small farmers with paperwork they cannot possibly manage.  新规让小农户背上他们根本应付不了的文书负担。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burden</td><td>/ˈbɜːrdən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使负重担；负担</td><td>burden him with debt<br>让他背上债务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They burdened the young manager with impossible sales targets.  他们让这位年轻经理背上无法完成的销售指标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encumber</td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍；妨碍</td><td>encumber the project<br>妨碍这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many rules encumber small businesses in this country.  太多规定阻碍了这个国家的小企业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 freight</td><td>/ˈfreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>装货于；货运</td><td>freight a ship with grain<br>给船装满谷物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The barge was freighted with coal for the northern mills.  驳船装满了运往北方工厂的煤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lade</td><td>/ˈleɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装载，装货</td><td>lade a cart with hay<br>给车装满干草</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers laded the wagon with heavy sacks of flour.  工人们把一袋袋沉重的面粉装上马车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lumber</td><td>/ˈlʌmbər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>笨拙地行动；使负担（从而拖累）</td><td>lumber across the yard<br>笨拙地穿过院子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old bear lumbered across the yard toward the garbage cans.  那头老熊笨拙地穿过院子朝垃圾桶走去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impose</td><td>/ɪmˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>强加，征收</td><td>impose a heavy tax<br>征收重税</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court imposed a strict curfew on the district.  法院对该区强加了严格宵禁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inflict</td><td>/ɪnˈflɪkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使遭受，施加</td><td>inflict damage on<br>对……造成损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The storm inflicted serious damage on coastal villages.  风暴对沿海村庄造成严重破坏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tax</td><td>/ˈtæks/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使负重担；课税</td><td>tax one's patience<br>考验某人的耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long delay taxed even the calmest passengers.  长时间延误让最镇定的乘客也难以承受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weight</td><td>/ˈweɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>重要性，影响力；重量</td><td>carry great weight<br>极有影响力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her opinion carries considerable weight with the committee.  她的意见在委员会里很有分量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disburden</del></td><td>/dɪsˈbɜːrdən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>卸下负担；解除烦恼</td><td>disburden oneself of worries<br>卸下心中的忧虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He finally disburdened himself of the secret he had kept for years.  他终于卸下了多年来藏着的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discharge</td><td>/dɪsˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>释放；解雇</td><td>discharge the prisoner<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court discharged the prisoner immediately after the new evidence appeared.  新证据出现后，法庭立即释放了那名囚犯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disencumber</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈkʌmbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使摆脱累赘，卸去负担</td><td>disencumber a ship of cargo<br>卸掉船上的货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She disencumbered herself of the heavy coat and sat down.  她脱掉厚重的外套坐了下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unburden</td><td>/ənˈbɜːrdən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>解除负担，倾吐</td><td>unburden oneself to a friend<br>向朋友倾吐心事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She unburdened herself of the secret she had kept for years.  她把保守多年的秘密倾吐了出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unlade</del></td><td>/ʌnˈleɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>卸货，卸下</td><td>unlade the cargo hold<br>卸空货舱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dockers began to unlade the freighter at first light.  码头工人天一亮就开始给货轮卸货。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unload</td><td>/ʌnˈloʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>卸货，卸下；倾诉</td><td>unload boxes from the van<br>从货车上卸下箱子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They unloaded all the furniture before the rain started.  他们在下雨前把家具都卸完了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>safeguard</b></td><td>/ˈseɪfɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>保护措施</td><td>a legal safeguard<br>法律保障</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new rules act as a safeguard against careless lending.  新规定是对草率放贷的一道保障。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aegis</td><td>/ˈiːdʒəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>保护，庇护</td><td>under the aegis of<br>在……的庇护下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The talks continued under the aegis of the United Nations.  会谈在联合国的主持庇护下继续。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caution</td><td>/ˈkɔːʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>谨慎；警告，告诫</td><td>proceed with caution<br>谨慎行事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guide urged caution on the icy mountain path.  向导叮嘱在结冰的山路上要谨慎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defense</td><td>/dɪˈfens/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>辩护，辩解；防御</td><td>in his own defense<br>为他自己辩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He said nothing in his own defense at the hearing.  在听证会上他没有为自己作任何辩解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fail-safe</td><td>/ˈfeɪlseɪf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有故障保险的，万无一失的</td><td>a fail-safe system<br>有故障保险的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reactor has a fail-safe system that shuts it down.  反应堆有一套故障保险系统可将其关闭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 guard</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>保护；警卫</td><td>guard against loss<br>防范损失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two dogs guard the warehouse every night.  两条狗每晚守卫这座仓库。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 palladium</td><td>/pəˈleɪdiəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>保障，保护物</td><td>a palladium of liberty<br>自由的保障</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A free press is often called the palladium of democracy.  自由的新闻界常被称为民主的保障。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precaution</td><td>/priˈkɔːʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>预防措施</td><td>take safety precautions<br>采取安全预防措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a precaution they stored extra water and batteries.  作为预防，他们储备了额外的水和电池。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preventive</td><td>/prɪˈventɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>预防的；预防措施</td><td>preventive measures<br>预防措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular inspections are the cheapest preventive measure available.  定期检查是现有最便宜的预防措施。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 security</td><td>/sɪˈkjʊrəti/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>安全，保障</td><td>a sense of security<br>安全感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new fence gave the family a real sense of security.  新围栏给这家人带来真正的安全感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bulwark</td><td>/ˈbʊlwərk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>堡垒，防御物</td><td>a bulwark against tyranny<br>抵御暴政的堡垒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A free press is a bulwark against official abuse.  自由的新闻界是抵御官方滥权的堡垒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cover</td><td>/ˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，遮盖；覆盖物</td><td>cover up the truth<br>掩盖真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They tried to cover up the truth with a vague statement.  他们试图用含糊的声明掩盖真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defend</td><td>/dɪˈfend/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>保卫，辩护</td><td>defend the border<br>保卫边境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soldiers defended the bridge for three days.  士兵们守卫这座桥三天之久。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fence</td><td>/ˈfens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>栅栏，篱笆；围起</td><td>a wooden fence<br>木栅栏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They painted the wooden fence around the garden last summer.  去年夏天他们给花园周围的木栅栏刷了漆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fend</td><td>/ˈfend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>抵挡，照料</td><td>fend for oneself<br>自己照顾自己</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He learned to fend off criticism with humor.  他学会用幽默挡开批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 keep</td><td>/ˈkiːp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遵守，坚持；保留</td><td>keep a promise<br>守约，履行承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He always keeps his promises, however inconvenient they are.  无论多不方便，他总是守约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 protect</td><td>/prəˈtekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保护，防护</td><td>protect from harm<br>保护免受伤害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick gloves protect your hands from the cold.  厚手套保护你的双手免受寒冷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 screen</td><td>/skriːn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>掩护物，屏障；掩护，遮蔽</td><td>a screen of trees<br>一道树木屏障</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A thick screen of trees hid the old house from the road.  一道浓密的树木屏障把老屋从路上遮住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secure</td><td>/sɪˈkjʊr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>牢固的，安全的</td><td>a secure connection<br>安全的连接</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Make sure the ladder is secure before you start climbing.  开始攀爬前确保梯子牢固。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shield</td><td>/ˈʃiːld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>保护，遮挡；盾</td><td>shield one's eyes<br>遮住眼睛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised a hand to shield her eyes from the glare.  她举手遮挡刺眼的强光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ward</td><td>/ˈwɔːrd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>守护，挡开；病房</td><td>ward off danger<br>挡开危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Garlic was once used to ward off illness.  大蒜曾被用来抵挡疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assail</td><td>/əˈseɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，严厉批评</td><td>assail a policy<br>抨击某项政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Opponents assailed the plan as reckless and unfair.  反对者抨击该计划轻率且不公。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assault</td><td>/əˈsɔːlt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>攻击，袭击</td><td>launch an assault<br>发动攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops launched a night assault on the fortress.  部队对要塞发动了夜间进攻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attack</td><td>/əˈtæk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>攻击，抨击</td><td>attack his motives<br>攻击他的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rival scholars attacked the credibility of her new evidence.  对立的学者抨击她新证据的可信度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sage</b></td><td>/seɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>智者</td><td>an ancient sage<br>古代的智者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers walked for days to ask the old sage for advice.  村民们走了好几天去向那位老智者求教。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expert</td><td>/ˈekspərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>专家；熟练的，专业的</td><td>a leading expert<br>顶尖专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They called in an expert to examine the cracked foundation.  他们请来专家检查开裂的地基。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illuminati</td><td>/ɪˌluːmɪˈnɑːti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>智者</td><td>the illuminati of the age<br>当代的智者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The salon gathered the illuminati of eighteenth-century Paris.  这个沙龙聚集了十八世纪巴黎的智者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 master</td><td>/ˈmæstər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>大师，名家；主人</td><td>a master of disguise<br>伪装大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a master of the craft could carve such fine details.  只有行家高手才能雕出这般精细的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mentor</td><td>/ˈmentɔːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>教导，指导</td><td>mentor young engineers<br>指导年轻工程师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Senior researchers mentor young engineers throughout their first two years at the laboratory.  资深研究员在年轻工程师入职头两年里全程指导他们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 savant</td><td>/sæˈvɑːnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>博学之士，学者</td><td>a quiet savant<br>沉静的博学之士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The quiet savant spent forty years cataloguing insects in a small museum.  那位沉静的博学之士在小博物馆里花了四十年为昆虫编目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scholar</td><td>/ˈskɑːlər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>学者，博学之人</td><td>a distinguished scholar<br>杰出的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture drew scholars from four different countries.  这场讲座吸引了四个国家的学者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dolt</td><td>/doʊlt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>笨蛋</td><td>call him a dolt<br>骂他是笨蛋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not call him a dolt simply because he asked an obvious question.  别只因为他问了个显而易见的问题就骂他笨蛋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fool</td><td>/ˈfuːl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>傻瓜，蠢人；愚弄</td><td>make a fool of oneself<br>让自己出丑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt like a fool after believing the obvious lie.  信了那个明显的谎言后，他觉得自己像个傻瓜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 idiot</td><td>/ˈɪdiət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>白痴，蠢人</td><td>act like an idiot<br>表现得像蠢人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only an idiot would drive that fast in fog.  只有蠢人才会在雾中开那么快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 simpleton</td><td>/ˈsɪmpltən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>（缺乏常识的）笨蛋</td><td>treat him as a simpleton<br>把他当傻子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They treated the new clerk as a simpleton until he corrected their figures.  他们把新来的职员当傻子，直到他改正了他们的数字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>salient</b></td><td>/ˈseɪliənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>显著的，最突出的</td><td>the salient features<br>最突出的特征</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please summarize the salient points of the report in a single page.  请把报告最突出的要点概括在一页之内。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conspicuous</td><td>/kənˈspɪkjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>显而易见的；吸引人的</td><td>conspicuous consumption<br>炫耀性消费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His bright red jacket made him conspicuous in the gray winter crowd.  他鲜红的夹克让他在灰暗的冬日人群中格外显眼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noticeable</td><td>/ˈnoʊtəsəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>显而易见的，明显的</td><td>a noticeable change<br>一个明显的变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a noticeable pause before he answered the question.  他回答问题前有一个明显的停顿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outstanding</td><td>/ˌaʊtˈstændɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的，突出的</td><td>an outstanding performance<br>出色的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an outstanding performance in her very first concert.  她在首场音乐会上表现出色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prominent</td><td>/ˈprɑːmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的；显眼的</td><td>a prominent local politician<br>当地知名的政治人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her name appears in a prominent place on the donor list.  她的名字出现在捐赠名单的显眼位置。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remarkable</td><td>/rɪˈmɑːrkəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非凡的，引人注目的</td><td>a remarkable achievement<br>非凡的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Finishing the bridge in a year was a remarkable achievement.  一年建成这座桥是非凡的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 striking</td><td>/ˈstraɪkɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人注目的，显著的</td><td>a striking resemblance<br>惊人的相似</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a striking contrast between the two sisters' styles.  两姐妹的风格形成鲜明的对比。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconspicuous</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈspɪkjuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不显眼的</td><td>remain inconspicuous<br>保持不显眼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He chose an inconspicuous seat at the back of the hall.  他在大厅后排选了个不显眼的座位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unnoticeable</td><td>/ˌʌnˈnoʊtɪsəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不易察觉的</td><td>almost unnoticeable change<br>几乎察觉不到的变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The repair is so neat it is unnoticeable from a distance.  修补得如此整齐，从远处看根本看不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>salubrious</b></td><td>/səˈluːbriəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的</td><td>a salubrious mountain climate<br>有益健康的山地气候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors sent him to a salubrious mountain village to recover his strength.  医生把他送到有益健康的山村去恢复体力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 good</td><td>/ˈɡʊd/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有利的，好的</td><td>a good opportunity<br>有利的机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This is a good moment to renegotiate the contract.  这是重新谈判合同的有利时机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 healthy</td><td>/ˈhelθi/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健康的，有益健康的</td><td>a healthy diet<br>健康的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Walking daily is a healthy habit at any age.  每天散步在任何年龄都是健康习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restorative</td><td>/rɪˈstɔːrətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的</td><td>a restorative sleep<br>恢复元气的睡眠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long walk by the sea proved wonderfully restorative after weeks of stress.  数周压力之后，在海边长时间散步格外有益身心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salutary</td><td>/ˈsæljəteri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的；有利的，利好的</td><td>a salutary lesson<br>有益的教训</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The failed launch had a salutary effect on the careless habits of the team.  那次发射失败对团队的马虎习惯起了有益的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tonic</td><td>/ˈtɑːnɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>滋补的，有益健康的</td><td>a tonic effect<br>滋补的功效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A week in the mountains had a tonic effect on her.  在山里待一周对她起了滋补强身的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wholesome</td><td>/ˈhoʊlsəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益身心健康的</td><td>wholesome home-cooked food<br>有益健康的家常饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children grew strong on wholesome food and plenty of fresh air.  孩子们靠有益健康的食物和充足的新鲜空气长得很结实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debilitating</td><td>/dəˈbɪləˌteɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>使人虚弱的</td><td>a debilitating illness<br>使人虚弱的疾病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A debilitating fever kept him in bed for weeks.  一场使人虚弱的高烧让他卧床数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deleterious</td><td>/ˌdeləˈtɪriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>deleterious effects on health<br>对健康的有害影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long night shifts had a deleterious effect on the nurses' health.  长期夜班对护士们的健康产生了有害影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 noxious</td><td>/ˈnɑːkʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，有毒的；产生强烈厌恶的</td><td>noxious fumes<br>有毒的烟气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Noxious fumes from the factory forced the school to close for two days.  工厂排出的有毒烟气迫使学校停课两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 virulent</td><td>/ˈvɪrələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶毒的，充满敌意的；毒性强的</td><td>a virulent personal attack<br>恶毒的人身攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist launched a virulent attack on the new mayor.  这位专栏作家对新市长发起了恶毒的攻击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>salutary</b></td><td>/ˈsæljəteri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的；有利的，利好的</td><td>a salutary lesson<br>有益的教训</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The failed launch had a salutary effect on the careless habits of the team.  那次发射失败对团队的马虎习惯起了有益的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 good</td><td>/ˈɡʊd/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有利的，好的</td><td>a good opportunity<br>有利的机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This is a good moment to renegotiate the contract.  这是重新谈判合同的有利时机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 healthy</td><td>/ˈhelθi/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健康的，有益健康的</td><td>a healthy diet<br>健康的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Walking daily is a healthy habit at any age.  每天散步在任何年龄都是健康习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restorative</td><td>/rɪˈstɔːrətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的</td><td>a restorative sleep<br>恢复元气的睡眠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long walk by the sea proved wonderfully restorative after weeks of stress.  数周压力之后，在海边长时间散步格外有益身心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salubrious</td><td>/səˈluːbriəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的</td><td>a salubrious mountain climate<br>有益健康的山地气候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors sent him to a salubrious mountain village to recover his strength.  医生把他送到有益健康的山村去恢复体力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tonic</td><td>/ˈtɑːnɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>滋补的，有益健康的</td><td>a tonic effect<br>滋补的功效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A week in the mountains had a tonic effect on her.  在山里待一周对她起了滋补强身的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wholesome</td><td>/ˈhoʊlsəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益身心健康的</td><td>wholesome home-cooked food<br>有益健康的家常饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children grew strong on wholesome food and plenty of fresh air.  孩子们靠有益健康的食物和充足的新鲜空气长得很结实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 advantageous</td><td>/ˌædvənˈteɪdʒəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有利的，有益的</td><td>an advantageous position<br>有利的位置</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax rules proved advantageous to small local businesses.  新税收规定对本地小企业很有利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 benefic</td><td>/bəˈnɛfɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益的，有利的</td><td>a benefic influence<br>有益的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform had a benefic effect on rural schools.  这项改革对农村学校产生了有益影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beneficent</td><td>/bɪˈnefɪsnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（对个人或社会）有益的</td><td>a beneficent influence<br>有益的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her long service had a beneficent influence on the whole village.  她多年的服务对整个村子产生了有益的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 benignant</del></td><td>/bɪˈnɪɡnənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，宽厚的</td><td>a benignant smile<br>慈祥的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old abbot listened with a benignant expression on his face.  老修院长面带慈祥的神情倾听着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 favorable</td><td>/ˈfeɪvərəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有利的</td><td>favorable conditions<br>有利的条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Favorable weather allowed the harvest to finish a week early.  有利的天气让收割提前一周完成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 friendly</td><td>/ˈfrendli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友好的，亲切的</td><td>a friendly welcome<br>友好的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shopkeeper was friendly and remembered every regular customer.  店主很友好，记得每位常客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 helpful</td><td>/ˈhelpfəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有帮助的；乐于助人的</td><td>a helpful suggestion<br>有益的建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your notes were extremely helpful when I revised the report.  我修改报告时你的笔记非常有帮助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kindly</td><td>/ˈkaɪndli/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>和善的；亲切地</td><td>a kindly old man<br>一位和善的老人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A kindly neighbor carried her groceries up the steep stairs.  一位和善的邻居帮她把杂货搬上陡楼梯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profitable</td><td>/ˈprɑːfətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有利可图的；有益的</td><td>a profitable business<br>赚钱的生意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bakery finally became profitable in its third busy year.  这家面包店在忙碌的第三年终于开始赚钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debilitating</td><td>/dəˈbɪləˌteɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>使人虚弱的</td><td>a debilitating illness<br>使人虚弱的疾病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A debilitating fever kept him in bed for weeks.  一场使人虚弱的高烧让他卧床数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deleterious</td><td>/ˌdeləˈtɪriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>deleterious effects on health<br>对健康的有害影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long night shifts had a deleterious effect on the nurses' health.  长期夜班对护士们的健康产生了有害影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 noxious</td><td>/ˈnɑːkʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，有毒的；产生强烈厌恶的</td><td>noxious fumes<br>有毒的烟气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Noxious fumes from the factory forced the school to close for two days.  工厂排出的有毒烟气迫使学校停课两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 virulent</td><td>/ˈvɪrələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶毒的，充满敌意的；毒性强的</td><td>a virulent personal attack<br>恶毒的人身攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist launched a virulent attack on the new mayor.  这位专栏作家对新市长发起了恶毒的攻击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bad</td><td>/ˈbæd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶意的；坏的，糟的</td><td>bad intentions<br>不良的居心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was no bad feeling between the two old rivals.  两位老对手之间并没有恶意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disadvantageous</td><td>/ˌdɪsædvənˈteɪdʒəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不利的，有害的</td><td>a disadvantageous position<br>不利的处境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax is disadvantageous to small family farms.  新税对小型家庭农场不利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfavorable</td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪvərəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不利的，不赞成的</td><td>unfavorable conditions<br>不利的条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unfavorable weather delayed the launch by two full days.  不利的天气使发射延迟了整整两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfriendly</td><td>/ʌnˈfrendli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不友好的，冷淡的</td><td>an unfriendly stare<br>不友好的注视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waiter gave us an unfriendly look and walked away.  服务员不友好地看了我们一眼便走开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unhelpful</td><td>/ənˈhelpfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无益的，帮不上忙的</td><td>an unhelpful reply<br>毫无帮助的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clerk gave a vague and thoroughly unhelpful answer.  职员给出一个含糊而毫无帮助的答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unprofitable</td><td>/ʌnˈprɑːfɪtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无利可图的，徒劳的</td><td>an unprofitable branch<br>无利可图的分店</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They closed three unprofitable branches last spring.  去年春天他们关闭了三家无利可图的分店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>salutation</b></td><td>/ˌsæljuˈteɪʃn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>致敬，问候</td><td>a salutation to the winners<br>向获胜者致敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd offered a warm salutation to the returning champions.  人群向归来的冠军们热情致敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accolade</td><td>/ˈækəleɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>赞扬，荣誉；奖励</td><td>win the highest accolade<br>赢得最高荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received accolades from critics on both continents.  这部小说获得了两大洲评论家的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 citation</td><td>/saɪˈteɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>表彰，嘉奖；引文</td><td>a citation for bravery<br>英勇嘉奖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She received a citation for bravery after the rescue.  救援之后她获得了英勇嘉奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commendation</td><td>/ˌkɑːmənˈdeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>表扬，嘉奖</td><td>receive a commendation for bravery<br>因勇敢获得嘉奖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer received a commendation for rescuing the trapped driver.  这名警官因救出被困司机而获嘉奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 eulogium</del></td><td>/juˈloʊdʒiəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>颂词，赞美之辞</td><td>deliver a warm eulogium<br>致热情的颂词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old professor delivered a graceful eulogium for his student.  老教授为学生致了一篇优雅的颂词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eulogy</td><td>/ˈjuːlədʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>悼词；颂词，赞辞</td><td>deliver a moving eulogy<br>致感人的悼词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She delivered a moving eulogy at her grandfather's funeral.  她在祖父的葬礼上致了感人的悼词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 homage</td><td>/ˈhɑːmɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>尊敬，敬意</td><td>pay homage to<br>向…致敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands came to pay homage to the poet on her ninetieth birthday.  数千人在诗人九十岁生日那天前来致敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hymn</td><td>/hɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赞歌，赞美诗</td><td>sing a hymn<br>唱赞美诗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole congregation stood up to sing a hymn of thanksgiving.  全体教众站起身来唱一首感恩的赞美诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 paean</td><td>/ˈpiːən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>欢乐颂，赞扬</td><td>a paean to freedom<br>对自由的赞歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review read like a paean to the young violinist and her talent.  那篇评论读来像是对这位年轻小提琴手及其才华的赞歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 panegyric</td><td>/ˌpænəˈdʒɪrɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赞颂之词，颂文</td><td>deliver a long panegyric<br>发表一篇长颂词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The retirement dinner ended with a long panegyric to the old professor.  退休晚宴以对这位老教授的长篇颂词结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tribute</td><td>/ˈtrɪbjuːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>称颂，颂词</td><td>pay tribute to her work<br>对她的工作表示称颂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Colleagues paid warm tribute to her forty years of patient teaching.  同事们对她四十年的耐心教学致以热情的颂扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>salvage</b></td><td>/ˈsælvɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>抢救，打捞</td><td>salvage the wreckage<br>打捞残骸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Divers tried to salvage cargo from the sunken ship.  潜水员试图从沉船中打捞货物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rescue</td><td>/ˈreskju/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>解救，营救</td><td>rescue the trapped miners<br>营救被困矿工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Helicopters rescued twelve people from the flooded village.  直升机从被淹的村庄救出十二人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retrieve</td><td>/rɪˈtriːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>寻回，找回</td><td>retrieve lost data<br>找回丢失的数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer managed to retrieve the files from the damaged hard drive.  工程师成功从损坏的硬盘中找回了文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 save</td><td>/ˈseɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拯救，挽救；节省</td><td>save him from danger<br>把他从危险中救出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lifeguard saved two swimmers from the strong current.  救生员从急流中救出两名游泳者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abandon</td><td>/əˈbændən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>放纵，尽情；放弃</td><td>with reckless abandon<br>肆意放纵地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She danced with wild abandon at the wedding reception.  她在婚宴上尽情忘我地跳舞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 desert</td><td>/dɪˈzɜːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抛弃，遗弃</td><td>desert one's post<br>擅离职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain refused to desert his crew in the storm.  船长拒绝在风暴中抛下船员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forsake</td><td>/fərˈseɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底放弃，抛弃</td><td>forsake one's friends<br>抛弃自己的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would never forsake the people who raised him.  他绝不会抛弃养育他的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>salve</b></td><td>/sæv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，缓解</td><td>salve one's conscience<br>减轻内心的不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave money to the school to salve his uneasy conscience.  他向学校捐钱，以减轻内心的不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balm</td><td>/bɑːm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>香油，止痛膏；安慰物</td><td>a balm for grief<br>抚慰悲伤之物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her quiet letters were a balm for his grief that winter.  那年冬天，她安静的信件是抚慰他悲伤之物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 becalm</del></td><td>/bɪˈkɑːm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平静，使停滞不动</td><td>becalmed by light winds<br>因风力太小而停滞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The yacht lay becalmed for two days without any breeze.  游艇因无风停滞了两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compose</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使镇定；组成；创作</td><td>compose oneself<br>让自己镇定下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a breath to compose himself before speaking.  他深吸一口气让自己镇定下来才开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lull</td><td>/lʌl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>相对平静时期，间隙；使镇静，使安心</td><td>a lull in the fighting<br>战斗中的间隙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief lull in the fighting allowed medics to reach the wounded.  战斗中短暂的间隙让医护人员得以接近伤员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lullaby</td><td>/ˈlʌləbaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>摇篮曲；唱摇篮曲使入睡</td><td>sing a gentle lullaby<br>唱一首轻柔的摇篮曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sang a gentle lullaby until the restless baby finally fell asleep.  她唱着轻柔的摇篮曲，直到不安的婴儿终于睡着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quiet</td><td>/ˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>安静的；平静的</td><td>keep the room quiet<br>保持房间安静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library stays quiet even during exam week.  即使在考试周，图书馆也保持安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 settle</td><td>/ˈsetəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>解决，平息；定居</td><td>settle the dispute<br>解决争端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides finally settled their dispute out of court.  双方最终在庭外解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 still</td><td>/ˈstɪl/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>寂静无声的；寂静</td><td>the still of night<br>夜晚的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The air was still and the lake looked like glass.  空气静止无风，湖面像玻璃一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tranquilize</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəˌlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使平静，给…服镇静剂</td><td>tranquilize the animal<br>给动物注射镇静剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vet had to tranquilize the frightened horse.  兽医不得不给这匹惊慌的马注射镇静剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agitate</td><td>/ˈædʒɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>煽动，激起；使不安</td><td>agitate for reform<br>鼓动改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students agitated for changes to the exam system.  学生们为改革考试制度而奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 discompose</del></td><td>/ˌdɪskəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安；使混乱</td><td>discompose the audience<br>让观众不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing seemed to discompose the veteran pilot, not even engine failure.  似乎没什么能让这位老飞行员心慌，连引擎故障也不行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disquiet</td><td>/dɪsˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>不安，忧虑；使不安</td><td>growing disquiet<br>日益增长的不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is growing disquiet among parents about the new curriculum.  家长中对新课程的不安情绪日益增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disturb</td><td>/dɪˈstɜːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打扰，扰乱；使不安</td><td>disturb the peace<br>扰乱安宁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please do not disturb the birds while they are nesting.  鸟类筑巢时请不要打扰它们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perturb</td><td>/pərˈtɜːrb/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安，使烦扰</td><td>perturb the balance<br>扰乱平衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden change of plan perturbed everyone on the team.  计划的突然改变让全队的人都心神不宁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upset</td><td>/əpˈset/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使心烦，使不安；打乱</td><td>upset the schedule<br>打乱日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of the closure upset the staff more than management expected.  停业的消息比管理层预想的更让员工不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vex</td><td>/veks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使烦恼，使恼怒</td><td>vex the teacher<br>使老师恼怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant interruptions vexed the teacher throughout the long afternoon lesson.  不断的打扰让老师在漫长的下午课上十分恼怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sanctify</b></td><td>/ˈsæŋktɪfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使神圣，使圣化</td><td>sanctify the temple ground<br>使庙地成为圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bishop came to sanctify the ground where the chapel would stand.  主教前来使这片将建礼拜堂的土地成为圣地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consecrate</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsəˌkreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使神圣；奉献</td><td>consecrate the new church<br>为新教堂祝圣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bishop consecrated the small stone church last Sunday morning.  主教上周日上午为这座小石教堂祝圣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hallow</td><td>/ˈhæloʊ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>尊敬，把…视为神圣</td><td>hallow the ancient site<br>将古址奉为神圣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Generations of pilgrims have hallowed this quiet mountain spring.  世代朝圣者把这泓山泉视为神圣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 desecrate</td><td>/ˈdesɪkreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>亵渎，玷污</td><td>desecrate a holy shrine<br>亵渎神圣的殿堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals desecrated the old cemetery and overturned a dozen stones.  破坏者亵渎了那座老墓园，推倒了十几块墓碑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 profane</td><td>/prəˈfeɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>亵渎</td><td>profane a sacred place<br>亵渎圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals profaned the temple by scrawling slogans on its walls.  破坏者在庙墙上乱涂标语，亵渎了圣殿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sanctimonious</b></td><td>/ˌsæŋktɪˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假装虔诚的</td><td>a sanctimonious tone<br>假装虔诚的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lectured us in a sanctimonious tone about virtues he clearly did not practice.  他用假装虔诚的语气向我们大谈他自己显然并不践行的美德。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 canting</td><td>/ˈkæntɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假装虔诚的，伪善的</td><td>canting hypocrisy<br>假虔诚的伪善</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His canting speech about virtue fooled nobody.  他关于美德的假虔诚演讲骗不了任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deceiving</td><td>/dɪˈsiːvɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>欺骗的，骗人的</td><td>a deceiving appearance<br>骗人的外表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The calm surface was deceiving, for the current ran fast.  平静的水面骗人，水流其实很急。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hypocritical</td><td>/ˌhɪpəˈkrɪtɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚伪的</td><td>hypocritical behavior<br>虚伪的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is hypocritical to demand honesty while hiding your own mistakes.  一边要求诚实一边隐瞒自己的错误，这很虚伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 devout</td><td>/dɪˈvaʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虔诚的，笃信的</td><td>a devout believer<br>虔诚的信徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His devout aunt attended the little chapel every single morning.  他虔诚的姑妈每天早晨都去那间小教堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 religious</td><td>/rɪˈlɪdʒəs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虔诚的，宗教的</td><td>religious devotion<br>宗教上的虔诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her religious upbringing shaped every choice she made.  她的宗教家教影响了她的每个选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pious</td><td>/ˈpaɪəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（信仰上）虔诚的</td><td>a pious old woman<br>虔诚的老妇人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pious old woman attended the early service every single morning.  那位虔诚的老妇人每天早上都参加早祷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sanction</b></td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accredit</td><td>/əˈkredɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>认可，授权；归功于</td><td>accredit the university<br>认可该大学资质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board finally accredited the new nursing program.  委员会终于认可了这个新的护理专业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 approbate</del></td><td>/ˈæprəbeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞许，认可</td><td>approbate the decision<br>认可这一决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board approbated the merger after a long private debate.  经过长时间闭门辩论，董事会认可了这次合并。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authorize</td><td>/ˈɔːθəˌraɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>授权，批准，委托</td><td>authorize the payment<br>批准这笔付款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only the director can authorize spending above that limit.  只有主管能批准超出该限额的开支。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 certify</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证明，认证</td><td>certify the results as valid<br>证明结果有效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An independent lab must certify that the water is safe.  必须由独立实验室证明水质安全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confirm</td><td>/kənˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认，进一步确定</td><td>confirm the findings<br>证实这些发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Later tests confirmed what the first team had suspected.  后来的检测证实了第一个团队的猜测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 finalize</td><td>/ˈfaɪnəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>最终确定，敲定</td><td>finalize the agreement<br>敲定协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides hope to finalize the agreement before December.  双方希望在十二月前敲定协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 formalize</td><td>/ˈfɔːrməˌlaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使正式化，使规范化</td><td>formalize the agreement<br>使协议正式化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms decided to formalize their loose partnership.  两家公司决定把松散的合作正式化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 license</td><td>/ˈlaɪsns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>许可，认可；自由</td><td>a license to practice<br>执业许可证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally obtained a license to practice medicine in three different states.  她终于取得了在三个不同州行医的执业许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ratify</td><td>/ˈrætɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（官方地）认可，批准</td><td>ratify the peace treaty<br>批准和平条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senate voted to ratify the peace treaty after weeks of debate.  经过数周辩论，参议院投票批准了和平条约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warrant</td><td>/ˈwɔːrənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保证，担保；使有正当理由</td><td>warrant the quality<br>保证质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory warrants the quality of every tool it sells abroad.  这家工厂为其出口的每件工具的质量提供保证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interdict</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈdɪkt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>禁止；拦截；封锁</td><td>interdict arms shipments<br>禁止武器运输</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Naval patrols were sent to interdict arms shipments along the coast.  海军巡逻队被派去拦截沿海的军火运输。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prohibit</td><td>/proʊˈhɪbət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>禁止，阻止</td><td>prohibit the sale of<br>禁止销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city prohibits overnight parking on this narrow street.  该市禁止在这条窄街上过夜停车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 proscribe</td><td>/proʊˈskraɪb/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>禁止，排斥</td><td>proscribe a political party<br>取缔某个政党</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new regime moved quickly to proscribe every opposition political party.  新政权迅速采取行动取缔所有反对党。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sanctuary</b></td><td>/ˈsæŋktʃueri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>庇护所；圣殿</td><td>seek sanctuary in a church<br>到教堂寻求庇护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The refugees found sanctuary in a small church near the border.  难民们在边境附近的一座小教堂找到了庇护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 asylum</td><td>/əˈsaɪləm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>政治庇护；收容所，避难所</td><td>grant political asylum<br>给予政治庇护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family was granted political asylum after two years of waiting.  这家人在等待两年之后获得了政治庇护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cover</td><td>/ˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，遮盖；覆盖物</td><td>cover up the truth<br>掩盖真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They tried to cover up the truth with a vague statement.  他们试图用含糊的声明掩盖真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harbor</td><td>/ˈhɑːrbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>藏匿，庇护；心怀（怨恨）</td><td>harbor a deep grudge<br>心怀深深怨恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family was accused of harboring a fugitive for several months.  这家人被指控藏匿一名逃犯数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haven</td><td>/ˈheɪvn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>避难所，安全的地方</td><td>a safe haven<br>安全的避难所</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old library became a quiet haven for students during exam week.  考试周里，那座旧图书馆成了学生们安静的避难所。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 port</td><td>/ˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>避风港，港口</td><td>a safe port in a storm<br>风暴中的安全港</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The battered ship finally reached port before dawn.  受损的船在黎明前终于抵港。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refuge</td><td>/ˈrefjudʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>避难所；庇护，慰藉</td><td>take refuge from the storm<br>躲避风暴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The travelers took refuge in a shepherd's stone hut.  旅人们在牧羊人的石屋里避难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shelter</td><td>/ˈʃeltər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>避难所；庇护</td><td>seek shelter from rain<br>找地方避雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They took shelter under a bridge until the hail stopped.  他们在桥下躲避，直到冰雹停下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>showcase</b></td><td>/ˈʃoʊkeɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>展示（优点）</td><td>showcase new talent<br>展示新人才</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The festival will showcase new talent from villages across the province.  这场节庆将展示来自全省各村的新人才。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brandish</td><td>/ˈbrændɪʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（带有威胁性地）挥舞</td><td>brandish a sword<br>挥舞长剑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The angry pirate began to brandish a sword above his head.  愤怒的海盗开始在头顶挥舞长剑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disport</del></td><td>/dɪˈspɔrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>嬉戏，娱乐</td><td>disport oneself on the beach<br>在海滩嬉戏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children disported themselves in the shallow water all afternoon.  孩子们整个下午都在浅水里嬉戏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhibit</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪbɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>展出；显示，表现出</td><td>exhibit great courage<br>表现出极大勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children often exhibit no fear of strangers at all.  幼童常常对陌生人完全不表现出畏惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expose</td><td>/ɪkˈspoʊz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露；使暴露</td><td>expose the fraud<br>揭露欺诈行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two journalists exposed the fraud after months of quiet investigation.  两名记者经过数月暗中调查揭露了这起欺诈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flash</td><td>/ˈflæʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>闪光；闪耀，闪现</td><td>a flash of lightning<br>一道闪电</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A light flashed briefly in the window across the street.  街对面的窗子里闪了一下光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flaunt</td><td>/ˈflɔːnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>炫耀，夸耀</td><td>flaunt one's wealth<br>炫耀财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He liked to flaunt his new watch at every meeting.  他喜欢在每次会议上炫耀他的新表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parade</td><td>/pəˈreɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>炫耀；游行</td><td>parade one's knowledge<br>炫耀自己的学识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She never paraded her achievements before her classmates.  她从不在同学面前炫耀成绩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 show off</td><td>/ʃoʊ ˈɔːf/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>炫耀，卖弄</td><td>show off in front of others<br>在别人面前炫耀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy climbed the wall just to show off to his friends.  那男孩爬上墙只是为了向朋友炫耀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trot out</td><td>/trɑt ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>端出（老套说法）；炫示</td><td>trot out the same excuse<br>老调重弹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Politicians trot out the same tired promises every election season.  每逢选举，政客们都端出同样陈旧的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conceal</td><td>/kənˈsiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>conceal the truth<br>隐瞒真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness tried to conceal the truth from the police.  证人试图向警方隐瞒真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hide</td><td>/ˈhaɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>hide the evidence<br>藏匿证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hid the letters in a drawer beneath her sweaters.  她把信藏在毛衣下面的抽屉里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 secrete</td><td>/sɪˈkriːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>隐藏；分泌</td><td>secrete the documents<br>把文件藏起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver had secreted the documents beneath the front seat.  司机把文件藏在了前排座椅下面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sanguine</b></td><td>/ˈsæŋɡwɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观的，确信的</td><td>sanguine about the future<br>对未来乐观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remains sanguine about the harvest despite three weeks without rain.  尽管三周无雨，她对收成依然乐观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assured</td><td>/əˈʃʊrd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自信的；有保证的</td><td>an assured manner<br>自信的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an assured performance despite the huge audience.  尽管观众众多，她的表演依然从容自信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confident</td><td>/ˈkɑːnfədənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自信的，确信的</td><td>confident of success<br>对成功有信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was confident that his team would recover.  教练确信他的队伍会恢复过来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doubtless</td><td>/ˈdaʊtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>副词</td><td>无疑地，肯定地</td><td>doubtless the best choice<br>无疑是最佳选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan will doubtless face strong opposition in the council.  这项计划无疑会在议会遭到强烈反对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 implicit</td><td>/ɪmˈplɪsɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不言而喻的，心照不宣的</td><td>an implicit agreement<br>心照不宣的协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Club members share an implicit agreement to split all travel costs.  俱乐部成员之间有一个心照不宣的约定：平摊所有旅行开销。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 positive</td><td>/ˈpɑːzətɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，明确的；积极的</td><td>positive proof<br>确凿的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I am positive that I locked the back door.  我确定我锁了后门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 doubtful</td><td>/ˈdaʊtfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以确定的，可疑的</td><td>of doubtful value<br>价值可疑的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is doubtful whether the bridge can carry such loads.  这座桥能否承受如此重量还很难说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dubious</td><td>/ˈduːbiəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满不定性的，值得怀疑的</td><td>a dubious claim<br>可疑的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The advertisement made a dubious claim that no serious scientist would support.  那则广告作出了一个没有严肃科学家会支持的可疑说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncertain</td><td>/ʌnˈsɜːrtn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不确定的，不明朗的</td><td>an uncertain future<br>不明朗的未来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The date of the meeting remains uncertain until funding is approved.  在资金获批前会议日期仍不确定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unsure</td><td>/ˌʌnˈʃʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不确定的，没把握的</td><td>unsure of the answer<br>对答案没把握</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She looked unsure when asked to explain the diagram.  被要求解释图表时她显得没有把握。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sanitary</b></td><td>/ˈsænəteri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>健康的，清洁的</td><td>sanitary conditions<br>卫生状况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen must stay sanitary during every shift.  厨房在每个班次都必须保持清洁卫生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aseptic</td><td>/ˌeɪˈseptɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>消毒的，无菌的</td><td>aseptic surgical technique<br>无菌手术操作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Surgeons follow strict aseptic procedure before touching any instrument.  外科医生在接触任何器械前都遵守严格的无菌程序。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 germfree</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɜrmfri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无菌的，无病菌的</td><td>a germfree environment<br>无菌环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab maintains a germfree environment for breeding these mice.  实验室为饲养这些小鼠保持无菌环境。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hygienic</td><td>/haɪˈdʒiːnɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卫生的，清洁的</td><td>hygienic food handling<br>卫生的食品操作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hospitals require hygienic practices in every ward.  医院要求每个病区都遵守卫生规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sterile</td><td>/ˈsterəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫瘠的；无菌的</td><td>sterile medical instruments<br>无菌医疗器械</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Surgeons must use sterile instruments to prevent infection during any operation.  外科医生必须使用无菌器械，以防手术中感染。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 noxious</td><td>/ˈnɑːkʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，有毒的；产生强烈厌恶的</td><td>noxious fumes<br>有毒的烟气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Noxious fumes from the factory forced the school to close for two days.  工厂排出的有毒烟气迫使学校停课两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sap</b></td><td>/sæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>削弱，逐渐耗尽</td><td>sap his strength<br>削弱他的力量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks of night shifts sapped the energy of the young doctor completely.  数周的夜班彻底削弱了这位年轻医生的精力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debilitate</td><td>/dɪˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>debilitate the immune system<br>使免疫系统衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus gradually debilitates the immune system of healthy adults.  这种病毒会逐渐削弱健康成年人的免疫系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 devitalize</del></td><td>/diːˈvaɪtəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使失去活力</td><td>devitalize the local economy<br>使当地经济失去活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of neglect devitalized the once thriving downtown district.  多年的忽视使曾经繁荣的市中心失去活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enervate</del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enfeeble</del></td><td>/ɪnˈfiːbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱，使虚弱</td><td>enfeeble the patient<br>使病人虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long fever enfeebled him so much that walking hurt.  长期发烧使他虚弱到走路都疼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 etiolate</del></td><td>/ˈiːtiəleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使苍白，使衰弱</td><td>etiolate a plant<br>使植物变白衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Darkness will etiolate the seedlings within a week.  黑暗会在一周内使幼苗变得苍白衰弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prostrate</td><td>/ˈprɑːstreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>平躺的，俯卧的；使极度虚弱</td><td>lie prostrate on the ground<br>平躺在地上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhausted runner lay prostrate on the ground after crossing the line.  那名精疲力竭的跑者冲线后平躺在地上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dynamism</td><td>/ˈdaɪnəˌmɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，干劲</td><td>economic dynamism<br>经济活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's dynamism attracts young workers every year.  这座城市的活力每年吸引年轻劳动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energy</td><td>/ˈenərdʒi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>精力，活力；能量</td><td>full of restless energy<br>充满旺盛的精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children still had plenty of energy after the long hike.  长途徒步之后孩子们仍精力充沛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 esprit</td><td>/esˈpriː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>才智，活力</td><td>esprit de corps<br>团队精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her writing sparkles with wit and esprit.  她的文章闪耀着机智与才气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 get-up-and-go</td><td>/ˈɡɛt ʌp ənd ˈɡoʊ/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>干劲，进取心</td><td>plenty of get-up-and-go<br>十足的干劲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager has real get-up-and-go and works late.  新经理确实有干劲，常工作到很晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gusto</td><td>/ˈɡʌstoʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>热情，兴致</td><td>eat with gusto<br>津津有味地吃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children sang the old song with great gusto.  孩子们兴致高昂地唱着那首老歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 verve</td><td>/vɜːrv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，热情</td><td>play with verve<br>充满激情地演奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist played that difficult passage with remarkable verve.  这位年轻钢琴家以非凡的热情演奏了那段高难度乐章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vim</td><td>/vɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，精力</td><td>full of vim and vigor<br>精力充沛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At seventy he still worked with surprising vim and good humor.  七十岁的他干活仍精力惊人，心情愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitality</td><td>/vaɪˈtæləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，生命力</td><td>brim with vitality<br>充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old market still brims with vitality on early summer mornings.  夏日清晨，这座老市场依然充满活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bolster</td><td>/ˈboʊlstər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，增强</td><td>bolster his confidence<br>增强他的信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A word of praise from her would bolster his confidence enormously.  她一句赞扬就会极大地增强他的信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fortify</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加固；增强，强化</td><td>fortify a city wall<br>加固城墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The milk is fortified with extra vitamin D.  这种牛奶额外强化添加了维生素D。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invigorate</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪɡəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使精神，使强壮，鼓舞激励</td><td>invigorate the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk in cold air always invigorates my tired mind.  在冷空气中短暂散步总能让我疲惫的头脑重新振作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 beef up</td><td>/biːf ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，充实</td><td>beef up security measures<br>加强安保措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city beefed up security before the international summit began.  国际峰会开始前，该市加强了安保。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lethargy</td><td>/ˈleθərdʒi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>无生气，倦怠，昏睡</td><td>shake off his lethargy<br>摆脱他的倦怠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk helped him shake off his afternoon lethargy.  短短的散步帮他摆脱了午后的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 listlessness</td><td>/ˈlɪstləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倦怠，无精打采</td><td>an air of listlessness<br>一副无精打采的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange listlessness settled over the office in August.  八月里办公室弥漫着一种奇怪的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sluggishness</td><td>/ˈslʌɡɪʃnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟缓，无精打采</td><td>morning sluggishness<br>早晨的迟缓无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat explained the general sluggishness of the crew.  炎热解释了船员们普遍的无精打采。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 torpidity</del></td><td>/tɔːrˈpɪdəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟钝，麻木</td><td>sink into torpidity<br>陷入麻木迟钝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Winter torpidity kept the frogs buried in the mud.  冬日的麻木状态让青蛙埋在泥中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sapient</b></td><td>/ˈseɪpiənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>聪明的，有洞察力的</td><td>a sapient observation<br>有洞察力的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A few sapient remarks from the editor saved the entire chapter.  编辑几句有洞察力的话救了整整一章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discerning</td><td>/dɪˈsɜːrnɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有洞察力的，眼光敏锐的</td><td>a discerning listener<br>有鉴赏力的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wine appeals to discerning drinkers rather than casual buyers.  这款酒吸引有鉴赏力的饮者而非随意的买家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insightful</td><td>/ˈɪnˌsaɪtfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有洞察力的，有见识的</td><td>an insightful comment<br>有洞察力的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her insightful reading of the poem impressed the whole class.  她对这首诗富有洞见的解读让全班佩服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perceptive</td><td>/pərˈseptɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏锐的</td><td>a perceptive observation<br>敏锐的观察</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her perceptive comments revealed how carefully she had listened.  她敏锐的评论显示出她听得多么仔细。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prudent</td><td>/ˈpruːdnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的；小心谨慎的，审慎的</td><td>a prudent investment<br>一笔审慎的投资</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It would be prudent to save part of every paycheck.  每份工资都存下一部分才是明智之举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sagacious</td><td>/səˈɡeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>睿智的，有远见的</td><td>a sagacious adviser<br>睿智的顾问</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His sagacious advice saved the family business from certain ruin.  他睿智的建议救家族生意于必败之境。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sage</td><td>/seɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>智者</td><td>an ancient sage<br>古代的智者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers walked for days to ask the old sage for advice.  村民们走了好几天去向那位老智者求教。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foolish</td><td>/ˈfuːlɪʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愚蠢的，傻的</td><td>a foolish mistake<br>愚蠢的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was foolish of him to ignore the warning signs.  他忽视警示标志实在愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unperceptive</del></td><td>/ˌʌnpərˈsɛptɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏洞察力的</td><td>an unperceptive reader<br>缺乏洞察力的读者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only an unperceptive critic would miss the irony here.  只有缺乏洞察力的评论者才会漏掉这里的讽刺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unwise</td><td>/ʌnˈwaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不明智的，愚蠢的</td><td>an unwise decision<br>不明智的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Borrowing more money now would be deeply unwise.  现在再借钱是很不明智的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sarcasm</b></td><td>/ˈsɑːrkæzəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>讽刺，轻蔑</td><td>a hint of sarcasm<br>一丝讽刺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a faint note of sarcasm in his polite reply.  他礼貌的回答中带着一丝讽刺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affront</td><td>/əˈfrʌnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>侮辱，冒犯</td><td>an affront to dignity<br>对尊严的侮辱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark was an affront to everyone in the room.  他随口的一句话侮辱了房间里的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 barb</td><td>/bɑːrb/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>尖锐而严厉的批评</td><td>a sharp barb<br>尖刻的讥讽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her final barb about his cooking silenced the whole table.  她最后那句关于他厨艺的尖刻讥讽让整桌人都不说话了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indignity</td><td>/ɪnˈdɪɡnəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>侮辱，轻蔑</td><td>suffer the indignity of<br>遭受……的侮辱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He endured the indignity of being searched at the gate.  他忍受了在门口被搜身的侮辱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 offense</td><td>/əˈfens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>冒犯，得罪；犯罪</td><td>take offense at<br>因……而生气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took offense at his joke about her accent.  她因他拿她的口音开玩笑而生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slap</td><td>/ˈslæp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>侮辱；打耳光</td><td>a slap in the face<br>当面的侮辱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing the award felt like a slap to the whole team.  没得奖对整个团队像是一记耳光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slur</td><td>/slɜːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>含糊不清地说；诋毁，污辱</td><td>slur one's words<br>说话含糊不清</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was so tired that he began to slur his words.  他累得开始说话含糊不清。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sartorial</b></td><td>/sɑːrˈtɔːriəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服装的，衣着的</td><td>sartorial elegance<br>衣着上的优雅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ambassador was famous for his sartorial elegance and perfect manners.  那位大使以衣着优雅和举止得体闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sate</b></td><td>/seɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使饱足，充分满足</td><td>sate one's hunger<br>充分满足饥饿感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single bowl of thin soup was not enough to sate his hunger.  一碗清汤不足以满足他的饥饿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cram</td><td>/ˈkræm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>塞满，填塞；死记硬背</td><td>cram the drawer with socks<br>把抽屉塞满袜子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She crammed all her winter clothes into one small suitcase.  她把所有冬衣塞进一个小箱子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glut</td><td>/ɡlʌt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使过量，使充满</td><td>glut the market<br>使市场供过于求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap imports have glutted the local market and driven prices down sharply.  廉价进口货使当地市场供过于求，价格大幅下跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stuff</td><td>/ˈstʌf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>填满，塞饱</td><td>stuff oneself with food<br>把自己吃得饱胀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boys stuffed themselves with cake at the party.  男孩们在派对上把蛋糕吃得饱胀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surfeit</td><td>/ˈsɜːrfɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）过量，（使）饮食过度</td><td>a surfeit of information<br>信息过量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surfeit of information often leaves readers more confused than informed.  信息过量常让读者更困惑而非更明白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 satiate</td><td>/ˈseɪʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>（使）饱足（的），过分满足（的）</td><td>satiate his curiosity<br>充分满足他的好奇心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No amount of reading could satiate the curiosity of that boy about stars.  再多的阅读也无法满足那男孩对星星的好奇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 starve</td><td>/ˈstɑːrv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使饥饿，饿死</td><td>starve to death<br>饿死</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The siege starved the town for nearly two months.  围城让这座城镇饥饿了近两个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>satiate</b></td><td>/ˈseɪʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>（使）饱足（的），过分满足（的）</td><td>satiate his curiosity<br>充分满足他的好奇心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No amount of reading could satiate the curiosity of that boy about stars.  再多的阅读也无法满足那男孩对星星的好奇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 replete</td><td>/rɪˈpliːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满…的，富于…的</td><td>replete with errors<br>错误连篇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His essay was replete with spelling errors.  他的文章满是拼写错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sate</td><td>/seɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使饱足，充分满足</td><td>sate one's hunger<br>充分满足饥饿感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single bowl of thin soup was not enough to sate his hunger.  一碗清汤不足以满足他的饥饿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stuff</td><td>/ˈstʌf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>填满，塞饱</td><td>stuff oneself with food<br>把自己吃得饱胀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boys stuffed themselves with cake at the party.  男孩们在派对上把蛋糕吃得饱胀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surfeit</td><td>/ˈsɜːrfɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）过量，（使）饮食过度</td><td>a surfeit of information<br>信息过量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surfeit of information often leaves readers more confused than informed.  信息过量常让读者更困惑而非更明白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tantalize</td><td>/ˈtæntəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，挑逗，引诱</td><td>tantalize with promises<br>用承诺引诱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of roast meat tantalized the hungry travelers.  烤肉的香味挑逗着饥饿的旅人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>scandalous</b></td><td>/ˈskændələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引起反感的，丢脸的</td><td>a scandalous waste of money<br>令人反感的浪费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the project a scandalous waste of public money.  批评者称这个项目是对公共资金令人反感的浪费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abhorrent</td><td>/æbˈhɔːrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的</td><td>abhorrent to most people<br>令多数人厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The practice is abhorrent to anyone with basic decency.  这种做法令任何有基本良知的人厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abominable</td><td>/əˈbɑːmənəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可憎的，极糟的</td><td>abominable behavior<br>可憎的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His abominable behavior at dinner shocked the guests.  他晚餐时可憎的举止让客人震惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hideous</td><td>/ˈhɪdiəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常丑陋的</td><td>a hideous mask<br>丑陋可怕的面具</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They tore down the hideous concrete tower that blocked the view.  他们拆掉了那座挡住视野的丑陋混凝土塔楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 horrible</td><td>/ˈhɔːrəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可怕的，糟糕的</td><td>a horrible mistake<br>可怕的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soup tasted horrible but nobody dared say so.  汤的味道很糟，但没人敢说出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noisome</td><td>/ˈnɔɪsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶臭的；令人极厌恶的</td><td>a noisome odor<br>一股恶臭气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers refused to enter the noisome tunnel without masks.  工人们不戴口罩不肯进入那条恶臭的隧道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 odious</td><td>/ˈoʊdiəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可憎的，令人厌恶的</td><td>an odious task<br>讨厌的差事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Comparing the two writers seems both odious and pointless.  比较这两位作家显得可憎又无意义。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repellent repugnant</td><td>/rɪˈpɛlənt rɪˈpʌɡnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的，讨厌的</td><td>a repellent, repugnant sight<br>令人厌恶反感的景象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scene was repellent, even repugnant, to everyone who watched it.  这一场景令所有看到的人厌恶，甚至反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shocking</td><td>/ˈʃɑːkɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人震惊的；骇人的</td><td>shocking news<br>令人震惊的消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photographs revealed shocking conditions inside the old prison.  这些照片揭露了旧监狱内骇人的条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocuous</td><td>/ɪˈnɑːkjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的；不会引起敌意的</td><td>an innocuous question<br>一个无恶意的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What seemed an innocuous question turned out to reveal her deepest secret.  看似无恶意的问题最终却揭露了她最深的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inoffensive</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈfensɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，不讨人厌的</td><td>inoffensive background music<br>不讨人厌的背景音乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is bland but inoffensive enough for family viewing.  这部电影平淡，但足够温和，适合全家观看。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>satirize</b></td><td>/ˈsætəraɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>讽刺</td><td>satirize the government<br>讽刺政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cartoonist loved to satirize politicians who promised more than they delivered.  这位漫画家喜欢讽刺那些承诺多于兑现的政客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lampoon</td><td>/læmˈpuːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>讽刺</td><td>a savage political lampoon<br>犀利的政治讽刺作品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His savage political lampoon appeared on the front page of the weekly.  他犀利的政治讽刺作品登上了这份周刊的头版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pasquinade</del></td><td>/ˌpæskwəˈneɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>讽刺文，讽刺短诗</td><td>publish a pasquinade<br>发表一篇讽刺文</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An anonymous pasquinade mocking the mayor appeared on walls across the city.  一篇匿名讽刺市长的短文出现在全城的墙上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>saturate</b></td><td>/ˈsætʃəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使饱和，浸透</td><td>saturate the sponge<br>使海绵吸饱水</td></tr>
