<tr><td>↳近 remote</td><td>/rɪˈmoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷淡的，疏离的；遥远的</td><td>a remote expression<br>疏离的神情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His manner remained remote even among close family members.  即使在近亲之间他也显得疏离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retired</td><td>/rɪˈtaɪrd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>僻静的；退休的</td><td>a retired corner of the garden<br>花园里僻静的一角</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They read in a retired spot behind the hedge.  他们在树篱后一处僻静地方读书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sheltered</td><td>/ˈʃeltərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受保护的，遮风避雨的</td><td>a sheltered childhood<br>受呵护的童年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew up sheltered and knew little of the world outside.  她在呵护中长大，对外面的世界几乎一无所知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>secrete</b></td><td>/sɪˈkriːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>隐藏；分泌</td><td>secrete the documents<br>把文件藏起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver had secreted the documents beneath the front seat.  司机把文件藏在了前排座椅下面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bury</td><td>/ˈberi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>埋葬，掩埋</td><td>bury the treasure<br>埋藏宝藏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Squirrels bury nuts all over the garden every autumn.  松鼠每年秋天把坚果埋在花园各处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cache</td><td>/kæʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>囤货，藏货</td><td>a cache of weapons<br>一批藏匿的武器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police discovered a cache of weapons beneath the barn floor.  警方在谷仓地板下发现了一批藏匿的武器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conceal</td><td>/kənˈsiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>conceal the truth<br>隐瞒真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness tried to conceal the truth from the police.  证人试图向警方隐瞒真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ensconce</del></td><td>/ɪnˈskɑːns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安置，安顿；隐藏</td><td>ensconce oneself in<br>把自己安置在</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She ensconced herself in the armchair nearest the fire and opened her book.  她把自己安置在离火最近的扶手椅里，打开了书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 divulge</td><td>/daɪˈvʌldʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>泄漏（秘密）</td><td>divulge a secret<br>泄露秘密</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No employee may divulge a secret learned during the merger negotiations.  任何员工都不得泄露在并购谈判中得知的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 display</td><td>/dɪsˈpleɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>展示，陈列；显露</td><td>display the paintings<br>展出这些画作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum displays its rarest coins in a locked glass case.  博物馆把最珍稀的钱币陈列在上锁的玻璃柜里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exhibit</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪbɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>展出；显示，表现出</td><td>exhibit great courage<br>表现出极大勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children often exhibit no fear of strangers at all.  幼童常常对陌生人完全不表现出畏惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absorb</td><td>/əbˈzɔːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使全神贯注；吸收</td><td>be absorbed in the book<br>沉浸在书里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puzzle absorbed him completely for the whole afternoon.  这道谜题让他整个下午都全神贯注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sedate</b></td><td>/sɪˈdeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>淡定的，安静的</td><td>a sedate atmosphere<br>安静沉稳的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel keeps a sedate atmosphere that older guests clearly appreciate.  这家酒店保持着安静沉稳的气氛，年长客人显然很喜欢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collected</td><td>/kəˈlektəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇定的，冷静的</td><td>calm and collected<br>冷静镇定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stayed cool and collected throughout the tense negotiation.  整个紧张的谈判过程中她都保持冷静镇定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 composed</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊzd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇静的，沉着的</td><td>remain composed<br>保持镇静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stayed composed while answering the reporters' hostile questions.  她在回答记者敌意的提问时始终保持镇静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-possessed</td><td>/ˌselfpəˈzest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静沉着的，从容的</td><td>cool and self-possessed<br>冷静自持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at fifteen she was remarkably self-possessed in public.  即便才十五岁，她在公开场合也异常沉着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 serene</td><td>/səˈriːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平静的，安详的</td><td>a serene smile<br>安详的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She watched the serene surface of the lake at dawn without speaking.  她默默看着黎明时宁静的湖面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tranquil</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平静的，安宁的</td><td>a tranquil lake<br>平静的湖面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a tranquil afternoon reading beside the quiet river.  我们在静静的河边度过了一个安宁的下午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undisturbed</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈstɜːrbd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未受扰乱的，平静的</td><td>remain undisturbed<br>保持不受打扰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The papers lay undisturbed on the desk for years.  这些文件在桌上多年未被动过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unperturbed</td><td>/ˌʌnpərˈtɜːrbd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉着的，不慌乱的</td><td>unperturbed by criticism<br>对批评不为所动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seemed unperturbed by the sudden change of plan.  她对计划突变显得毫不慌乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unruffled</td><td>/ənˈrʌfəld/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇定的，平静的</td><td>unruffled by the noise<br>不为噪音所扰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained unruffled despite the angry shouting around him.  尽管周围怒吼声不断，他依然镇定自若。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agitated</td><td>/ˈædʒəˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>焦躁不安的，激动的</td><td>become visibly agitated<br>明显焦躁起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew agitated when the police refused to answer her questions.  警察拒绝回答她的问题时，她变得焦躁不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 discomposed</del></td><td>/ˌdɪskəmˈpoʊzd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>心慌的，不安的</td><td>look visibly discomposed<br>显得明显心慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The question left the speaker discomposed for a moment.  这个问题让演讲者一时慌了神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disturbed</td><td>/dɪˈstɜːrbd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不安的，受扰的</td><td>deeply disturbed by news<br>被消息深深扰乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was disturbed by the noise from the next room.  他被隔壁的噪音扰得不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flustered</td><td>/ˈflʌstərd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慌乱的，手足无措的</td><td>get flustered easily<br>容易慌乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew flustered when the lights went out.  灯熄灭时她变得慌乱起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perturbed</td><td>/pərˈtɜːrbd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>焦虑的，心烦意乱的</td><td>perturbed by the delay<br>因延误而心烦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seemed perturbed by the letter she had just opened.  她刚打开的那封信似乎让她心烦意乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upset</td><td>/əpˈset/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使心烦，使不安；打乱</td><td>upset the schedule<br>打乱日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of the closure upset the staff more than management expected.  停业的消息比管理层预想的更让员工不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sedentary</b></td><td>/ˈsednteri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固定不动的</td><td>a sedentary lifestyle<br>久坐不动的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of sedentary office work had weakened his back and shoulders.  多年久坐不动的办公工作削弱了他的背和肩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 migratory</td><td>/ˈmaɪɡrətɔːri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迁移的</td><td>migratory birds<br>候鸟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Migratory birds pause at this shallow lake before continuing south for the winter.  候鸟在继续南飞过冬前会在这片浅湖停歇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peripatetic</td><td>/ˌperipəˈtetɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巡游的，四处游历的</td><td>a peripatetic career<br>四处奔波的职业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peripatetic photographer rarely slept in the same city twice.  这位四处游历的摄影师很少在同一座城市住两次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>seduce</b></td><td>/sɪˈduːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使（背离正道），引诱</td><td>seduce someone into crime<br>引诱某人犯罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 High salaries seduced many teachers away from the classroom.  高薪诱使许多教师离开了课堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allure</td><td>/əˈlʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>吸引，诱惑</td><td>the allure of fame<br>名声的诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap rents allure young artists to this run-down district.  低廉的租金吸引年轻艺术家来到这个破旧街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decoy</td><td>/ˈdiːkɔɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>诱饵，圈套；诱骗</td><td>act as a decoy<br>充当诱饵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wooden duck served as a decoy to attract real birds.  木鸭子被用作诱饵来吸引真鸟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entice</td><td>/ɪnˈtaɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使，引诱</td><td>entice customers with discounts<br>用折扣吸引顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free samples were used to entice shoppers into the new store.  免费样品被用来引诱购物者进入新店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solicit</td><td>/səˈlɪsɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>请求，征求；招揽</td><td>solicit donations<br>募集捐款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity solicited funds from local businesses all winter.  该慈善机构整个冬天向本地企业募捐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tempt</td><td>/ˈtempt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱惑，引诱</td><td>tempt fate<br>冒险行事，玩火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of fresh bread tempted us into the little bakery.  新鲜面包的香味把我们诱进了那家小面包店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lead on</td><td>/liːd ˈɑːn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>引诱，误导（使抱幻想）</td><td>lead someone on<br>误导某人抱幻想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally realized he was leading her on with empty promises.  她终于意识到他在用空头承诺吊着她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repulse</td><td>/rɪˈpʌls/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使厌恶，排斥</td><td>repulsed by the smell<br>被那气味恶心到</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sight of the rotting fruit repulsed everyone standing near the crate.  腐烂水果的样子让站在箱子旁的每个人都感到恶心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ward off</td><td>/ˌwɔːrd ˈɔːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>挡开，避开</td><td>ward off temptation<br>抵挡诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wore a thick scarf to ward off the wind.  他戴了厚围巾以挡开寒风。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>sedulous</b></del></td><td>/ˈsedʒələs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤奋认真的</td><td>sedulous preparation<br>勤勉认真的准备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sedulous preparation showed in every answer she gave the examiners.  她勤勉认真的准备体现在给考官的每个回答中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assiduous</td><td>/əˈsɪdʒuəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤勉的，专心仔细的</td><td>assiduous attention to detail<br>对细节的专注仔细</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her assiduous attention to detail saved the project from disaster.  她对细节的专注仔细使项目免于失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diligent</td><td>/ˈdɪlɪdʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤勉的，辛勤的</td><td>a diligent student<br>勤奋的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A diligent editor checked every footnote before the manuscript went to the printer.  一位勤勉的编辑在书稿付印前核对了每一条脚注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 industrious</td><td>/ɪnˈdʌstriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤勉的，刻苦的</td><td>an industrious worker<br>勤劳的工人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The most industrious student in the class reviewed her notes every single night.  班里最勤奋的学生每晚都复习笔记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laborious</td><td>/ləˈbɔːriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰苦的；勤奋的</td><td>a laborious process<br>费力的过程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying the manuscript by hand proved a slow and laborious process.  手抄这份手稿是一个缓慢而费力的过程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occupied</td><td>/ˈɑːkjəˌpaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忙于…的，被占用的</td><td>fully occupied with work<br>完全忙于工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was occupied with the report all afternoon.  她整个下午都忙于那份报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tied-up</td><td>/ˈtaɪd ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忙得不可开交的；被占用的</td><td>tied-up all afternoon<br>整个下午都很忙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager is tied-up in meetings until late tonight.  经理要开会一直忙到今晚深夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 idle</td><td>/ˈaɪdl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闲置的，未使用的；懒散的</td><td>idle machines<br>闲置的机器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Half the factory's machines stood idle for most of last year.  这家工厂一半的机器去年大部分时间都闲置着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inactive</td><td>/ɪnˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不活动的，不活跃的</td><td>remain inactive for months<br>数月毫无动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The volcano has been inactive for over two hundred years.  这座火山已有两百多年没有活动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unbusy</del></td><td>/ʌnˈbɪzi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不忙的，空闲的</td><td>an unbusy afternoon<br>空闲的午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Call me on an unbusy morning and we can talk properly.  哪天上午不忙时打给我，我们好好聊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unemployed</td><td>/ˌʌnemˈplɔɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>失业的；未被利用的</td><td>unemployed factory workers<br>失业的工厂工人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has been unemployed since the plant closed last spring.  自去年春天工厂关闭后他一直失业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unoccupied</td><td>/ʌnˈɑːkjupaɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>空闲的；无人占用的</td><td>an unoccupied seat<br>无人就座的座位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The upstairs rooms remained unoccupied for the whole winter.  楼上的房间整个冬天都空着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>seemly</b></td><td>/ˈsiːmli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体的，合宜的</td><td>seemly conduct in public<br>公共场合得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher expects seemly conduct from her students in public places.  老师期望学生在公共场所举止得体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 befitting</td><td>/bɪˈfɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>适当的，相称的</td><td>a welcome befitting a hero<br>配得上英雄的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They gave him a farewell befitting a long and honest career.  他们为他举行了与漫长诚实的职业生涯相称的告别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decent</td><td>/ˈdiːsənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体的，正派的</td><td>decent and respectful behavior<br>得体而恭敬的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a decent man who always keeps his promises.  他是个正派的人，总是守承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decorous</td><td>/ˈdekərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体端正的</td><td>decorous behavior at dinner<br>晚餐时得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guests kept a decorous silence while the old bishop said grace.  老主教做餐前祷告时，客人们保持着得体的安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 genteel</td><td>/dʒenˈtiːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，不粗俗的</td><td>genteel manners<br>有教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her genteel accent betrayed an expensive and very traditional upbringing.  她文雅的口音透露出昂贵而传统的成长背景。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 respectable</td><td>/rɪˈspektəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>体面的，值得尊敬的</td><td>a respectable middle-class family<br>体面的中产家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They came from a respectable family with an excellent local reputation.  他们出身于当地声誉极佳的体面家庭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncouth</td><td>/ʌnˈkuːθ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的，无教养的</td><td>uncouth table manners<br>粗野的餐桌举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His uncouth remarks at dinner offended nearly everyone at the table.  他在晚餐上粗鲁的言辞几乎惹恼了同桌所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ribald</td><td>/ˈrɪbəld/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（举止、言语）下流粗俗的</td><td>ribald jokes<br>粗俗下流的笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old sailor entertained the crew with ribald songs late into the night.  老水手用粗俗下流的歌曲让船员们乐到深夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indecorous</td><td>/ɪnˈdekərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合乎礼节的，不得体的</td><td>indecorous remarks at the ceremony<br>典礼上不得体的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His indecorous jokes at the memorial service embarrassed nearly everyone in the room.  他在追悼会上不得体的玩笑让在场几乎所有人都很尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 improper</td><td>/ˌɪmˈprɑːpər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不适当的，不合礼节的</td><td>improper use of funds<br>资金使用不当</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was dismissed for improper conduct toward a colleague.  他因对同事行为不当而被解雇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inappropriate</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈproʊpriət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不适当的，不合适的</td><td>inappropriate behavior at work<br>工作中的不当行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing shorts to the interview was clearly inappropriate.  穿短裤去面试显然不合适。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indecent</td><td>/ɪnˈdiːsnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不得体的，下流的</td><td>indecent language in public<br>公开场合的下流言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comedian was fined for using indecent language on live television.  这位喜剧演员因在直播电视上使用下流言语被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indelicate</td><td>/ɪnˈdelɪkət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不雅的，粗俗的</td><td>an indelicate question<br>不雅的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Asking about her salary was an indelicate question at the party.  在聚会上问她的薪水是个不得体的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unbecoming</td><td>/ˌʌnbɪˈkʌmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不得体的，不合适的</td><td>conduct unbecoming an officer<br>不合军官身份的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such language is unbecoming in a public hearing.  这种言辞在公开听证会上不得体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 ungenteel</del></td><td>/ˌʌndʒɛnˈtiːl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不文雅的，粗俗的</td><td>an ungenteel manner<br>不文雅的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ungenteel jokes shocked the older guests.  他不文雅的玩笑让年长客人吃惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>segment</b></td><td>/ˈseɡˌment/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>分割；部分</td><td>segment the market<br>对市场进行细分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Marketers try to segment the audience by age and shopping habits.  营销人员试图按年龄和购物习惯细分受众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 member</td><td>/ˈmembər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>部分，肢体；成员</td><td>a member of the body<br>身体的一个部分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sculptor carved the figure's members with remarkable anatomical accuracy.  雕塑家以惊人的解剖精确度雕出了人像的各个肢体部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 partition</td><td>/pɑːrˈtɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>分割，划分</td><td>the partition of the estate<br>地产的分割</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brothers argued for years over the partition of the family land.  兄弟们为家族土地的分割争执了多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 portion</td><td>/ˈpɔːrʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一份，部分</td><td>a generous portion<br>分量很足的一份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They served a generous portion of rice with every dish.  每道菜都配了分量很足的米饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 section</td><td>/ˈsekʃən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>分割，划分；部分</td><td>section the material<br>把材料切分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher sectioned the class into four working groups.  老师把班级分成四个学习小组。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>self-abasement</b></td><td>/ˌsɛlf əˈbeɪsmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>自我贬低，自我羞辱</td><td>excessive self-abasement<br>过度的自我贬低</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His constant self-abasement made colleagues uncomfortable.  他不断的自我贬低让同事们不自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 self-asserting</td><td>/ˌself əˈsɜːrtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自我主张强的，好出头的</td><td>a self-asserting personality<br>好出头的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The self-asserting newcomer soon dominated every meeting.  这位好出头的新人很快主导了每次会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>selfabsorbed</b></del></td><td>/ˌselfəbˈsɔːrbd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自恋的，自私的</td><td>a selfabsorbed teenager<br>自我中心的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too selfabsorbed to notice that his friend had been crying all evening.  他太自我中心，没注意到朋友整晚都在哭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 narcissistic</td><td>/ˌnɑːrsɪˈsɪstɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自恋的</td><td>narcissistic self-regard<br>自恋的自我关注</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His narcissistic self-regard made honest conversation with him nearly impossible.  他自恋的自我关注让人几乎无法与他坦诚交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-centered</td><td>/ˌselfˈsentərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自我中心的，自私的</td><td>a self-centered child<br>自我中心的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is so self-centered that he never asks how others feel.  他极其自我中心，从不问别人的感受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-infatuated</td><td>/ˌsɛlf ɪnˈfætʃueɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自我迷恋的</td><td>a self-infatuated poet<br>自我迷恋的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The self-infatuated poet quoted himself all evening.  这位自恋的诗人整晚都在引用自己。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-obsessed</td><td>/ˌsɛlf əbˈsɛst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自我着迷的，只想着自己的</td><td>a self-obsessed celebrity<br>只想着自己的名人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her diary reads like the work of a self-obsessed teenager.  她的日记读来像自我着迷的少年所写。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-oriented</td><td>/ˌself ˈɔːrientɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>以自我为中心的</td><td>a self-oriented attitude<br>以自我为中心的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her self-oriented planning ignored what the team needed.  她以自我为中心的规划忽视了团队需求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-preoccupied</td><td>/ˌsɛlf priˈɑkjupaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>全神贯注于自身的</td><td>a self-preoccupied manner<br>只顾自己的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He listened in a self-preoccupied way, waiting to speak.  他心里只顾自己地听着，只等开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 self-forgetting</td><td>/ˌsɛlf fərˈɡɛtɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忘我的，无私的</td><td>self-forgetting devotion<br>忘我的奉献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her self-forgetting care for the patients moved the whole ward.  她对病人忘我的照护感动了整个病房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 selfless</td><td>/ˈselfləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无私的，不自私的</td><td>selfless devotion to others<br>对他人的无私奉献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors praised her selfless care for the elderly couple.  邻居们称赞她对那对老夫妇的无私照料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unselfish</td><td>/ʌnˈselfɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无私的，不自私的</td><td>unselfish devotion<br>无私的奉献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unselfish passing made the whole team better.  他无私的传球让整支球队更出色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>seminal</b></td><td>/ˈsemɪnl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>开创性的，影响深远的</td><td>a seminal study<br>一项开创性研究</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her seminal paper changed how biologists think about animal migration.  她开创性的论文改变了生物学家对动物迁徙的看法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 derivative</td><td>/dɪˈrɪvətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非原创的</td><td>a derivative style<br>缺乏原创的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reviewers called the album pleasant but derivative of earlier folk records.  评论者称这张专辑悦耳，却是对早期民谣唱片的模仿而无原创。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sensation</b></td><td>/senˈseɪʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轰动事件</td><td>cause a sensation<br>引起轰动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist caused a sensation at her very first public concert.  那位年轻钢琴家在首次公开演出上引起轰动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flash</td><td>/ˈflæʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>闪光；闪耀，闪现</td><td>a flash of lightning<br>一道闪电</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A light flashed briefly in the window across the street.  街对面的窗子里闪了一下光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 marvel</td><td>/ˈmɑːrvl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>惊叹，惊奇；令人惊奇的事物</td><td>marvel at the view<br>对景色惊叹不已</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Visitors still marvel at the vaulted ceiling of the cathedral after eight centuries.  八百年后，参观者仍对这座大教堂的拱顶惊叹不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 miracle</td><td>/ˈmɪrəkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>奇迹，不可思议之事</td><td>a medical miracle<br>医学上的奇迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was a miracle that everyone walked away from the crash.  所有人都从车祸中走了出来，真是奇迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 phenomenon</td><td>/fəˈnɑːməˌnɑːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>现象；非凡的人或事</td><td>a natural phenomenon<br>一种自然现象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists still cannot fully explain this strange phenomenon.  科学家仍无法完全解释这个奇怪现象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 portent</td><td>/ˈpɔːrtent/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>预兆，凶兆</td><td>a dark portent<br>阴暗的凶兆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers read the comet as a dark portent of war.  村民们把这颗彗星看作战争的阴暗凶兆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prodigy</td><td>/ˈprɑːdədʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>天才，奇才；奇事</td><td>a child prodigy<br>神童</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young violinist was hailed as a prodigy at eight.  这位年轻小提琴手八岁时就被誉为神童。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 splendor</td><td>/ˈsplendər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>壮丽，华美</td><td>in all its splendor<br>尽显其壮丽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The restored palace now stands in all its former splendor.  修复后的宫殿如今重现昔日的壮丽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unnoticed event</td><td>/ʌnˈnoʊtɪst ɪˈvɛnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无人注意的事件</td><td>an unnoticed event in history<br>历史上无人注意的事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What seemed an unnoticed event later changed the law.  当时看似无人注意的事件后来改变了法律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sift</b></td><td>/ˈsɪft/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>审查</td><td>sift through the evidence<br>细查证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investigators will sift through the evidence before naming any suspect.  调查人员会先细查证据，再点名任何嫌疑人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 audit</td><td>/ˈɔːdɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>审计，查核</td><td>audit the accounts<br>查核账目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An outside firm will audit the accounts each spring.  每年春天由外部公司查核账目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 examine</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæmɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>细查，仔细审视</td><td>examine the evidence closely<br>仔细审查证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scientist examined every slide under the microscope twice.  这位科学家把每张切片在显微镜下看了两遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oversee</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈsiː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>监督，监管</td><td>oversee the construction<br>监督施工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was hired to oversee the entire relocation.  她受聘监督整个搬迁工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 review</td><td>/rɪˈvjuː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>审查，复审</td><td>review the evidence<br>审查证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee will review each application in detail.  委员会将详细审查每份申请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 survey</td><td>/sərˈveɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>调查；勘测；概览</td><td>conduct a survey<br>开展一项调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We surveyed every household about their water use last summer.  去年夏天我们调查了每户人家的用水情况。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>showmanship</b></td><td>/ˈʃoʊmənʃɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>擅于表演、展示的技巧</td><td>pure showmanship<br>纯粹的表演技巧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His victory owed more to showmanship than to any real skill.  他的胜利更多靠的是表演技巧而非真本领。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grandstanding</td><td>/ˈɡrændˌstændɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>哗众取宠，出风头</td><td>political grandstanding<br>政治上的哗众取宠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator's speech was mere grandstanding for the cameras.  这位参议员的演讲只是对着镜头哗众取宠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sepulchral</b></td><td>/səˈpʌlkrəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴沉的；丧葬的</td><td>a sepulchral voice<br>阴沉低哑的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered in a sepulchral voice that chilled everyone at the table.  他用阴沉的嗓音回答，让桌旁的人都发冷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depressing</td><td>/dɪˈpresɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人沮丧的</td><td>a depressing forecast<br>令人沮丧的预测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rainy weather and empty streets felt deeply depressing.  阴雨天气和空荡的街道让人非常压抑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 desolate</td><td>/ˈdesələt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>荒凉的；孤苦凄凉的</td><td>a desolate landscape<br>荒凉的景色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The abandoned village looked desolate under the grey winter sky.  废弃的村庄在灰暗冬空下显得荒凉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dismal</td><td>/ˈdɪzməl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴沉的，凄凉的</td><td>a dismal failure<br>彻底的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dismal gray sky matched everyone's mood.  阴沉灰暗的天空与众人的心情相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dreary</td><td>/ˈdrɪri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调乏味的</td><td>a dreary afternoon<br>沉闷的下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a dreary afternoon indoors watching rain streak the windows.  我们在室内度过一个沉闷的下午，看着雨水划过窗玻璃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morose</td><td>/məˈroʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>grow morose and silent<br>变得忧郁沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After losing the election he grew morose and rarely left his apartment.  落选之后他变得忧郁，很少离开公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sullen</td><td>/ˈsʌlən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闷闷不乐的</td><td>a sullen silence<br>闷闷不乐的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sullen silence fell over the table when the topic was raised.  提到这个话题时，餐桌上陷入闷闷不乐的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 tenebrous</del></td><td>/ˈtenəbrəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>黑暗的，阴郁的</td><td>a tenebrous hallway<br>幽暗的走廊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They crept along the tenebrous passage with one candle.  他们举着一支蜡烛沿幽暗的走道潜行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 merry</td><td>/ˈmeri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢乐的，愉快的</td><td>a merry gathering<br>欢乐的聚会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The house was merry with music and laughter.  屋里充满音乐和笑声，十分欢乐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bright</td><td>/ˈbraɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快活的，开朗的；明亮的</td><td>a bright smile<br>开朗的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted every customer with a bright and genuine smile.  她用开朗真诚的笑容迎接每位顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheery</td><td>/ˈtʃɪri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愉快的，兴高采烈的</td><td>a cheery greeting<br>愉快的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cheery voice on the phone lifted his mood.  她电话里愉快的声音让他心情好转。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 comforting</td><td>/ˈkʌmfərtɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人安慰的，宽慰的</td><td>a comforting thought<br>一个令人安慰的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was comforting to know that help was only minutes away.  知道救援只有几分钟就到，这很让人安心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cordial</td><td>/ˈkɔːrdʒəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情友好的；令人振奋的</td><td>a cordial welcome<br>热情的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two leaders exchanged cordial greetings before the talks.  两位领导人在会谈前互致亲切的问候。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 festive</td><td>/ˈfestɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢乐的，节日的</td><td>a festive mood<br>欢乐的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole street took on a festive air by evening.  到傍晚整条街都染上了节日气息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heartwarming</td><td>/ˈhɑːrtˌwɔːrmɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>暖心的，感人的</td><td>a heartwarming story<br>暖心的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their reunion after ten years was heartwarming to watch.  他们十年后的重逢看着令人暖心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sunshiny</td><td>/ˈsʌnˌʃaɪni/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>阳光灿烂的</td><td>a sunshiny morning<br>阳光灿烂的早晨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was a sunshiny day, perfect for drying the laundry.  这是个阳光灿烂的日子，正适合晒衣服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>septic</b></td><td>/ˈseptɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>腐败的，感染的</td><td>a septic wound<br>感染化脓的伤口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cut turned septic after two days without treatment.  伤口两天未处理后感染化脓了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 free of infection</td><td>/friː əv ɪnˈfɛkʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感染的，洁净的</td><td>keep the wound free of infection<br>使伤口保持无感染</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Clean dressings keep the wound free of infection.  干净的敷料使伤口保持无感染。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>sequela</b></del></td><td>/sɪˈkwiːlə/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>后遗症，后续结果</td><td>a lasting sequela<br>持久的后遗结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Chronic fatigue was a common sequela of the illness.  慢性疲劳是这种疾病常见的后遗结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 precursor</td><td>/priːˈkɜːrsər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>先驱者，先导</td><td>a precursor to war<br>战争的先兆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising tensions along the border proved a precursor to open war.  边境紧张局势加剧，成了公开战争的先兆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sequester</b></td><td>/sɪˈkwestər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使隔绝，分离</td><td>sequester the jury<br>隔离陪审团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge decided to sequester the jury until a verdict was reached.  法官决定隔离陪审团直到作出裁决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insulate</td><td>/ˈɪnsəleɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使绝缘，使隔离，不受外界影响</td><td>insulate children from reality<br>使孩子隔绝现实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wealthy parents often try to insulate their children from everyday hardship.  富裕的父母常常试图让孩子隔绝日常的艰辛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seclude</td><td>/səˈkluːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使隔离，使隐居</td><td>seclude oneself from society<br>使自己与社会隔绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The writer secluded himself in a cabin for the winter.  作家整个冬天把自己隔离在小屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 segregate</td><td>/ˈseɡrəˌɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>隔离，分开</td><td>segregate recyclable waste<br>分离可回收垃圾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The law once segregated students by race in public schools.  法律曾在公立学校按种族隔离学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cut off</td><td>/kʌt ˈɔːf/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>隔离，切断</td><td>cut off from the world<br>与世隔绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Snow cut off the village for nearly a week.  大雪把村子隔绝了将近一周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 permit to mingle</td><td>/pərˈmɪt tə ˈmɪŋɡəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>允许交往混合</td><td>permit to mingle freely<br>允许自由交往</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guards would not permit the prisoners to mingle.  看守不允许犯人们互相交往。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 desegregate</td><td>/dɪˈseɡrəˌɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>取消种族隔离</td><td>desegregate the schools<br>取消学校的种族隔离</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ruling forced the state to desegregate its schools.  这项裁决迫使该州取消学校的种族隔离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 integrate</td><td>/ˈɪntəˌɡreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>整合，使成一体</td><td>integrate into the system<br>融入体系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school works hard to integrate new arrivals into class life.  学校努力让新来者融入班级生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reintegrate</td><td>/ˌriːˈɪntəɡreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使重新融入</td><td>reintegrate into society<br>重新融入社会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Programs help former inmates reintegrate into their communities.  项目帮助前囚犯重新融入社区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sere</b></td><td>/sɪr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>干枯的，凋萎的</td><td>a sere brown field<br>枯黄的田地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By August the meadow had turned sere and brown under the sun.  到八月，草地在阳光下变得干枯发黄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arid</td><td>/ˈærɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>枯燥无趣的；干燥的</td><td>an arid lecture<br>枯燥的讲座</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor's arid lecture emptied half the seats before the break.  这位教授枯燥乏味的讲座在课间前就空了一半座位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 droughty</del></td><td>/ˈdraʊti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>干旱的，缺水的</td><td>a droughty summer<br>干旱的夏天</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three droughty years ruined the wheat harvest.  三个干旱年头毁掉了小麦收成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thirsty</td><td>/ˈθɜːrsti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>渴望的；口渴的</td><td>thirsty for knowledge<br>渴求知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young reporter was thirsty for a really big story.  这位年轻记者渴望一条真正的大新闻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waterless</td><td>/ˈwɔːtərləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无水的，干涸的</td><td>a waterless valley<br>无水的山谷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They crossed a waterless plain for two long days.  他们花了漫长的两天穿越无水的平原。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lush</td><td>/lʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>茂盛的，繁茂的</td><td>lush vegetation<br>茂盛的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lush vegetation covered the valley floor after weeks of steady rain.  连续数周的降雨之后，茂盛的植被覆盖了谷底。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 damp</td><td>/ˈdæmp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>抑制，使沮丧；潮湿的</td><td>damp their enthusiasm<br>打消他们的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain did not damp our enthusiasm for the trip.  下雨并没有打消我们对这次旅行的热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dank</td><td>/dæŋk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴湿的</td><td>a dank cellar<br>阴湿的地窖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They stored the wine in a dank cellar beneath the kitchen.  他们把酒存放在厨房下面阴湿的地窖里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humid</td><td>/ˈhjuːmɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>潮湿的，湿润的</td><td>hot and humid weather<br>湿热的天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The air was so humid that our clothes never dried.  空气如此潮湿，我们的衣服总也干不了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 moist</td><td>/ˈmɔɪst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>微湿的，湿润的</td><td>keep the soil moist<br>保持土壤湿润</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cover the seeds and keep the soil moist for a week.  盖好种子并保持土壤湿润一周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wet</td><td>/ˈwet/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>湿的，潮的；弄湿</td><td>soaking wet clothes<br>湿透的衣服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My shoes got wet crossing the flooded street.  我的鞋在穿过被淹的街道时湿了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>serendipity</b></td><td>/ˌserənˈdɪpəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>意外发现珍奇（或称心）事物的本领</td><td>a moment of serendipity<br>意外收获的时刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pure serendipity led the researchers to a cure they were not seeking.  纯粹的意外机缘让研究者找到了他们并未寻求的疗法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>serene</b></td><td>/səˈriːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平静的，安详的</td><td>a serene smile<br>安详的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She watched the serene surface of the lake at dawn without speaking.  她默默看着黎明时宁静的湖面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arcadian</td><td>/ɑːrˈkeɪdiən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>田园牧歌的，恬静的</td><td>an arcadian retreat<br>恬静的田园隐居处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The valley offered an arcadian calm they had never known.  山谷带来他们从未体验过的田园宁静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 calm</td><td>/ˈkɑːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词、名词</td><td>平静的；使镇静</td><td>calm down quickly<br>很快平静下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a calm voice until the children settled.  她用平静的声音说话，直到孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hushed</td><td>/ˈhʌʃt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>寂静的，静下来的</td><td>a hushed room<br>静寂的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hushed audience waited for the pianist to begin playing.  安静的观众等着钢琴家开始演奏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peaceful</td><td>/ˈpiːsfəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和平的，平静的</td><td>a peaceful settlement<br>和平的解决方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides finally chose a peaceful path out of conflict.  双方最终选择了走出冲突的和平道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 placid</td><td>/ˈplæsɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静的，淡定的</td><td>a placid temperament<br>沉静的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His placid temperament made him ideal for this very stressful job.  他沉静的性情使他非常适合这份高压工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restful</td><td>/ˈrestfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宁静的，使人放松的</td><td>a restful atmosphere<br>宁静的氛围</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pale green walls make the room restful.  淡绿色的墙让房间显得宁静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 still</td><td>/ˈstɪl/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>寂静无声的；寂静</td><td>the still of night<br>夜晚的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The air was still and the lake looked like glass.  空气静止无风，湖面像玻璃一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tranquil</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平静的，安宁的</td><td>a tranquil lake<br>平静的湖面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a tranquil afternoon reading beside the quiet river.  我们在静静的河边度过了一个安宁的下午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 boisterous</td><td>/ˈbɔɪstərəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>喧嚷的，吵闹的</td><td>a boisterous crowd<br>喧闹的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A boisterous crowd filled the square outside the stadium.  一群喧闹的人挤满了体育场外的广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clamorous</td><td>/ˈklæmərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喧闹的；大声要求的</td><td>clamorous demands for reform<br>高声要求改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clamorous market made quiet conversation impossible.  喧闹的市场让人根本无法安静交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 clattery</del></td><td>/ˈklætəri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喧闹的，哐当作响的</td><td>a clattery kitchen<br>哐当作响的厨房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clattery old train kept everyone awake.  哐当作响的旧火车让所有人无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deafening</td><td>/ˈdefənɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>震耳欲聋的</td><td>deafening applause<br>雷鸣般的掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd's cheer was deafening as the winner appeared.  获胜者出现时人群的欢呼震耳欲聋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 raucous</td><td>/ˈrɔːkəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的，喧闹的</td><td>raucous laughter<br>刺耳的大笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Raucous laughter spilled out of the crowded pub.  刺耳的笑声从挤满人的酒吧里传出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 roistering</del></td><td>/ˈrɔɪstərɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喧闹作乐的</td><td>a roistering crowd<br>喧闹作乐的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A roistering party next door lasted until dawn.  隔壁喧闹的聚会一直闹到黎明。</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 romping</td><td>/ˈrɑːmpɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>嬉闹的，欢闹的</td><td>romping children<br>嬉闹的孩子们</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The romping puppies knocked over two chairs.  嬉闹的小狗撞倒了两把椅子。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rowdy</td><td>/ˈraʊdi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吵闹的，撒野的</td><td>a rowdy party<br>闹翻天的派对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rowdy fans kept the whole neighborhood awake.  吵闹的球迷让整个街区都睡不着。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tumultuous</td><td>/tuˈmʌltʃuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喧闹的；动乱的</td><td>tumultuous applause<br>雷鸣般的掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pianist returned to tumultuous applause from the standing crowd.  钢琴家返场时全场起立，掌声雷动。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unquiet</td><td>/ʌnˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不平静的，不安的</td><td>an unquiet mind<br>不安的心绪</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comedian's last joke drew uproarious laughter from the crowd.  喜剧演员最后一个笑话引来观众的哄堂大笑。</td></tr>
